*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍首个公众人物访谈语音数据集PDC，包含情感效价和认知模态标注，并提出目标发言人参与度（TSP）分类法及音频优先的说话人分离流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语料库缺乏针对公众人物访谈语音的联合情感与认知模态标注，且缺乏可靠的目标发言人识别方法，阻碍了公共话语研究。

Method: 收集998个视频，跨7个专业领域100位发言人；提出五类目标发言人参与度（TSP）标注体系（κ=0.616）；采用结合Whisper ASR和pyannote的音频优先说话人分离流程；句子分割与过滤后得186,642句。

Result: 构建了包含310万词的大规模标注语料库，发布开源工具、验证样本及完整处理管道，数据集托管于Hugging Face。

Conclusion: PDC为公共话语分析提供了高质量多模态资源，TSP分类法和音频优先流程可推广至其他语料库构建项目，促进跨领域研究。

Abstract: We introduce the \textbf{Public Discourse Corpus (PDC)}, the first dataset of public-figure interview speech jointly annotated for affective valence and epistemic modality. The corpus contains 998 videos from 100 speakers across seven professional domains, yielding 186,642 sentences (3.1 million words) after sentence segmentation and filtering. To ensure that all retained videos contain analyzable speech from the intended speaker, we introduce \textbf{Target Speaker Participation (TSP)}---a five-category annotation taxonomy with documented inter-annotator reliability ($κ= 0.616$)---as a key methodological contribution that any corpus construction project can adopt. Target-speaker turns are separated from interviewer and third-party speech through an \textbf{audio-first diarization pipeline} combining local Whisper ASR with pyannote speaker separation, released as an open-source implementation. We release the annotated corpus, the annotation tools, the cross-provider validation sample, and the complete processing pipeline. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ictchenbo/public-discourse-corpus.

</details>


### [51] [Lens: Bringing the Right Semantic Perspective into Focus for Training-Free Multimodal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20252)
*Xinran Liu,Shouqian Shi,Yixian Chen,Ruizhi Chen,Xin-Wei Yao,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Lens，一种无需训练的表示学习框架，通过语义视角锚定和上下文化短语读出，解决自回归模型中语义视角错位问题，在36个MMEB数据集上Precision@1达到63.9，超越同主干无训练基线10.2点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义激发方法无法可靠地将提取状态导向下游任务所需的语义视角，导致表示仍由显著输入内容主导，论文将此问题定性为语义视角错位，旨在使表示读出任务导向。

Method: 提出Lens框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 语义视角锚定，将任务所需视角与任务特定读出短语关联，指定后续提取位置的解释角色；2) 上下文化短语读出，将同一短语置于完整输入之后并聚合其标记状态，结合全上下文访问与锚定视角，实现任务条件下的证据整合与推理。

Result: 在无参数更新、架构修改或重排序情况下，Lens在36个MMEB数据集上总体Precision@1达到63.9，比最接近的同主干无训练嵌入基线高出10.2点。

Conclusion: Lens通过显式锚定语义视角并结合上下文化读出，有效解决了语义视角错位，实现了高质量的任务导向表示学习，无需任何训练即可大幅超越现有无训练基线。

Abstract: High-quality representations are essential for a wide range of downstream tasks. Dedicated embedding models are explicitly optimized for representation learning, yet their training data are often more limited in scale and diversity than the massive corpora used to pretrain modern large language models and multimodal large language models. Large-scale pretraining and instruction following enable autoregressive models to select relevant evidence, integrate multimodal information, and infer semantics under different task perspectives, creating a distinctive opportunity for training-free representation learning. However, our analysis reveals that existing semantic-elicitation methods do not reliably orient the extracted states toward the semantic perspective required by the downstream task. Consequently, the resulting representations often remain dominated by salient input content. We characterize this problem as semantic perspective misalignment and propose Lens, a training-free framework that makes representation readout task-directed. Semantic Perspective Anchoring associates the task-required perspective with a task-specific readout phrase, specifying the interpretive role of the positions later used for extraction. Contextualized Phrase Readout places the same phrase after the complete input and aggregates its token states, combining full-context access with the anchored perspective. The resulting representation reflects task-conditioned evidence integration and inference rather than a generic summary of salient content. Without parameter updates, architectural modification, or reranking, Lens achieves an overall Precision@1 of 63.9 across all 36 MMEB datasets, outperforming the closest same-backbone training-free embedding baseline by 10.2 points.

</details>


### [52] [Schema-Anchored Latent Reasoning for Semantic Parsing-Based Knowledge Base Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20398)
*Guangze Gao,Zixuan Li,Sikui Zhang,Chunfeng Yuan,Wenjuan Li,Bing Li,Xiaolong Jin,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模式锚定的潜在推理方法SALR，用于语义解析型知识库问答，通过在隐藏状态中生成连续思维并延迟显式逻辑形式决策，结合模式编码本对齐与反馈，在GrailQA和WebQSP上取得稳定提升，尤其在组合性问题上超越强基线TIARA 2.86 F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的语义解析方法在大规模异构知识库上，往往在中间推理步骤过早做出离散的模式元素（关系、类别）选择，导致错误决策传播并最终生成错误的逻辑形式。需要一种能延迟显式承诺、利用连续潜在推理并锚定模式信息的方法。

Method: SALR采用模式锚定的潜在推理：1) 在模型隐藏状态中生成连续思维进行多步推理，延迟显式逻辑形式决策；2) 构建知识库模式元素编码本，通过从金标准逻辑形式确定性派生的模式轨迹监督，将连续思维与编码本对齐；3) 将对齐后的模式编码作为后续推理步骤的输入，提供模式中介反馈，指导逻辑形式生成，无需显式文本推理轨迹。

Result: 在GrailQA和WebQSP基准上，SALR相对强基线持续取得整体增益；在GrailQA组合性问题上，比强基线TIARA高出2.86 F1点。进一步分析表明模式中介反馈影响逻辑形式生成，且模式信息可从潜在状态恢复。

Conclusion: SALR通过潜在推理与模式编码本对齐，有效缓解了早期离散模式决策的错误传播问题，提升了复杂知识库问答中逻辑形式生成的准确性，证明了隐式模式反馈优于显式文本推理轨迹的可行性。

Abstract: Semantic parsing (SP)-based knowledge base question answering aims to answer natural language questions by generating executable logical forms (LFs) over knowledge bases (KBs). When applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to this task, a key challenge over large, heterogeneous KBs is selecting question-related schema elements (i.e., relations and classes) and composing them into complex LFs. Recent LLM-based methods often make early discrete commitments to schema elements during intermediate reasoning, allowing incorrect intermediate schema decisions to propagate and finally result in incorrect LFs. To overcome this limitation, we propose SALR, a schema-anchored latent reasoning method for LF construction. It performs multi-step reasoning by generating continuous thoughts in the model's hidden states, thereby delaying the explicit commitment to LF decisions. To ground this latent reasoning process in the corresponding KB schema, SALR aligns continuous thoughts with a codebook of KB schema elements through an alignment objective supervised by schema traces deterministically derived from gold LFs. It then incorporates the aligned schema codes into inputs for subsequent reasoning steps. This schema-mediated feedback guides LF generation without requiring the model to emit an explicit textual reasoning trajectory. Experiments on GrailQA and WebQSP show that SALR achieves consistent overall gains over strong baselines. Notably, on compositional questions from GrailQA, SALR outperforms TIARA, a strong SP-based baseline, by 2.86 F1 points. Further analyses show that schema-mediated feedback affects LF generation and that schema information is recoverable from the latent states.

</details>


### [53] [Xeno-Interpretability: Investigating the Alien Minds of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20408)
*F. Pierucci,M. Bracale Syrnikov,M. Prandi,M. Galisai,F. Giarrusso,P. Bisconti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了「异质表征」(xeno-representations) 和「异质可解释性」(xeno-interpretability) 概念，探讨大语言模型内部可能存在人类无概念对应的表征结构，主张将可解释性研究重心从寻找人类概念转向发现和刻画模型原生的表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的可解释性研究主要依赖人类已有概念（如真实性、拒答、欺骗、人格、有害性等），但模型内部表征空间可能远大于人类有限描述空间，存在无法用人类概念充分表达的内部区分。

Method: 区分人类可解释语义空间与异质语义空间；证明模型内部可能区分的空间大于人类描述空间；将实验识别与语义解释分离——内部表征可被可复现地定位、几何刻画、因果操控并关联下游行为，即使其语义内容无法用人类术语充分表达；勾勒识别异质表征的实证研究方案。

Result: 论证了异质表征的存在性和重要性，提出了异质可解释性研究框架，指出模型原生表征可能在多智能体交互中传播稳定，却仅通过人类可读沟通部分可见，对AI安全和多智能体系统有深远影响。

Conclusion: 异质可解释性将可解释性目标从在模型中寻找人类概念，转向发现和刻画模型原生的、可能以不可预测方式影响行为的表征结构，这是理解和控制先进AI系统的关键方向。}

Abstract: Large language models are usually interpreted through concepts that humans already possess: truthfulness, refusal, deception, personality, harmfulness, and related categories. This paper asks whether models may also represent and use distinctions for which no adequate human concept exists. We call such internal structures xeno-representations, and their study xeno-interpretability. We distinguish the human-interpretable semantic space from the xeno-semantic space: the region of model-native representations for which no adequate human conceptual counterpart is available. We show that the space of possible internal distinctions in an LLM is substantially larger than the space available through finite human descriptions. We then separate experimental identification from semantic interpretation: an internal representation may be reproducibly located, geometrically characterized, causally manipulated, and linked to downstream behaviour even when its semantic content cannot be adequately expressed in human terms. On this basis, we sketch an empirical programme to identify xeno-representations. We finally examine the implications for AI safety and multi-agent systems, where model-native representations may propagate and stabilize across interacting agents while remaining only partially visible through human-readable communication. Xeno-interpretability therefore shifts the aim of interpretability from finding human concepts inside models toward discovering and characterizing the representational structures that are native to the models themselves and might affect their behaviour in unpredictable ways.

</details>


### [54] [Stress-testing Alignment Midtraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20412)
*Sid Baines,Jonathan Bostock,Maria Angelica Martinez,Andrew Draganov,David Africa,Daniel Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了对齐中期训练（AMT）的有效性，通过大规模实验（高达110亿参数、10亿中期训练token）评估其核心假设，发现AMT在简单场景下能引导模型动机，但微调数据中极少量的竞争动机会抵消其效果；且规则泛化要求演示必须出现在中期训练或后训练数据中。结论认为公开证据不足以支持AMT能解决强AI对齐的核心难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐前沿模型时，无法在后训练中演示所有部署环境下的期望行为，模型需在后训练分布外泛化。AMT被提出作为解决方案，通过在大量对齐相关文档上继续预训练来鼓励泛化，但缺乏公开证据证明其有效性。

Method: 识别中期训练的几个核心假设，并在大规模设置下（高达110亿参数模型、10亿中期训练token）进行实证评估。具体研究两类场景：1）后训练数据在两种动机间模糊时，AMT能否引导动机；2）要求AI遵循多条规则但仅演示子集时，规则泛化的条件。

Result: 1）在简单版本中，AMT能引导模型朝向特定动机；但微调数据中极少量（微小比例）的竞争动机演示会完全抹除AMT效果。2）规则泛化要求演示必须存在于中期训练或后训练数据中，否则无法稳健学习。其他发现进一步质疑AMT的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于实验结果，作者认为目前的公开证据不足以让我们有信心断言中期训练能解决对齐强大AI系统的核心困难。

Abstract: When aligning frontier models through post-training techniques, it is not possible to directly demonstrate all of the behaviours we want a model to exhibit in all possible deployment environments; our model must generalise outside of the post-training distribution. One proposed solution is alignment midtraining (AMT), which continues pretraining on large volumes of alignment-relevant documents to encourage generalisation in later stages of training.
  Despite the prominence of AMT as an alignment approach, there is limited public evidence for its effectiveness. To resolve this, we identify several assumptions around midtraining and evaluate them across scale: up to 110 billion-parameter models and 1 billion midtraining tokens. For instance, we study a scenario where post-training data is ambiguous between two possible motivations. We find that midtraining can steer the model's motivation in simple versions of this setting. However, the presence of a tiny fraction of finetuning data which suggests a competing motivation erases the effects of AMT. We also study scenarios in which we want an AI to follow a number of rules, but only demonstrate a subset of them. We find that demonstrations must be present either in midtraining or post-training datasets for these rules to be robustly learned.
  Based on these and other findings, we do not believe that there is sufficient public evidence for us to confidently state that midtraining can address the core difficulties inherent in aligning powerful AI systems.

</details>


### [55] [Edustories: A Collection of Real-world Case Studies from Classroom Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20484)
*Michal Štefánik,Jan Nehyba,Jirina Karasova,Martin Fico,Lucie Škarková,Markéta Košatková,David Kosatka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Edustories数据集，包含1,492个教师撰写的真实课堂案例研究，用于评估大语言模型预测教学干预成效的能力。实验表明，当前最强模型准确率为58%，低于人类专家的64%，揭示了AI作为教师助手的局限与潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI教育研究多关注个性化学生辅助，但全球大多数教学实践仍发生在集体课堂环境中。因此需要构建数据集，支持研究AI在集体教学场景下的辅助能力。

Method: 构建Edustories数据集，收集小学和高中真实课堂中学生挑战性行为、教学干预及结果的案例研究；利用该数据集评估四大语言模型家族的最新模型预测干预成效的能力，并与人类专家评估对比。

Result: 最强模型预测准确率达到58%，而人类专家为64%，存在明显差距；当前模型在预测课堂干预结果上尚未达到人类专家水平。

Conclusion: AI在集体教学辅助方面仍存在局限性，但已展现出作为执业教师助手的潜力，未来研究可致力于缩小这一差距。

Abstract: Despite the widely recognized potential of AI in education, most prior work has focused on individualized student assistance. In contrast, the majority of educational practice worldwide still takes place in collective classroom settings. To enable researchers to study AI assistance in collective teaching, we introduce Edustories, a dataset of 1,492 teacher-written case studies describing real elementary and high-school classroom situations involving challenging student behavior, pedagogical interventions, and their outcomes. Among many other applications, Edustories enables evaluating LLMs' ability to predict the success of teacher interventions, crucial for providing practicing teachers with useful feedback. Comparing the latest models from four language-model families against expert assessments, we find that current models fall short of human expertise in predicting classroom outcomes; the strongest models reach 58% accuracy compared to 64% of human experts. This gap highlights both the limitations and the emerging potential of AI as assistants for practicing teachers.

</details>


### [56] [Relational Attention for Data-Efficient Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20530)
*Adrian Brasoveanu,Ece Takmaz,Jakub Dotlačil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Relational BabyLM，结合双重注意力 Transformer (DAT) 与 Next-Latent Prediction 目标，在 BabyLM 2026 严格赛道 (100M 词) 中取得第 6 名总分、第 3 名 NLP 任务分，超越 GPT-2 基线且获得最高 EWoK 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索认知动机的归纳偏置——分离对象级词汇特征与结构/关系信息——能否在数据受限的语言建模中提升样本效率与泛化能力，并验证关系注意力 (RA) 与潜在状态压缩目标的协同作用。

Method: 1. 架构：用双重注意力 Transformer (DAT) 替代标准自注意力，分离感官词汇特征与关系信息，提供完全 RA、RCA、DisRCA 三种变体。2. 训练目标：引入 Next-Latent Prediction (NextLat) 目标，鼓励隐藏状态增量压缩历史为稠密信念状态。3. 符号检索：基于 RoPE 的相对符号机制，无需额外参数即可匹配学习型符号库。

Result: 严格赛道 (100M 词) 总榜第 6/55，NLP 任务子集第 3/55；两个最强模型在多数基准上超越 GPT-2，其一获严格赛道最高 EWoK 分数。架构对结构语言泛化起主导作用，目标次之但显著；10M 词时三种 RA 变体表现接近，100M 词时完全 RA 优势显现。

Conclusion: DAT 将关系注意力与对象级注意力解耦，结合 NextLat 目标，显著提升数据效率与结构泛化；RoPE 符号检索零参数实现等效性能。架构设计是关键，训练目标提供补充增益，为认知启发的高效语言建模提供有力实证。

Abstract: We present Relational BabyLM, a system submission to the BabyLM 2026 challenge that combines two cognitively motivated inductive biases in a single decoder-only Transformer. Architecturally, we replace standard self-attention with a Dual Attention Transformer (DAT), which separates the routing of object-level ("sensory") lexical features from structural/relational information (Altabaa and Lafferty, 2025; Altabaa et al., 2024; Webb et al., 2024; Kerg et al., 2022; Webb et al., 2021). Relational attention (RA) disentangled from self-attention greatly increases data efficiency and out-of-training-sample generalization on purely relational tasks, but language modeling requires object-level and relational information to be integrated as well as disentangled, and RA-based LMs have remained largely unexplored. BabyLM's data-constrained training and comprehensive evaluation is an ideal testing ground for whether that data efficiency transfers. As a training intervention, we add a Next-Latent Prediction (NextLat; Teoh et al. 2026) objective that encourages hidden states to compress history incrementally into a dense belief state. Architecture is the dominant factor for structural linguistic generalization; the objective is secondary but still significant. DAT's three relational attention types (full RA vs. the simpler RCA and DisRCA variants) are largely interchangeable at 10M words; full RA pulls ahead at 100M. We also introduce a novel symbol-retrieval mechanism (RoPE-based, as opposed to learned, relative symbols) that matches learned symbol libraries while adding no parameters. On the strict (100M-word) track, our best model ranks 6th of 55 overall and 3rd of 55 on the leaderboard's NLP-task subset at the time of writing; our two strongest models outperform the GPT-2 baseline on most benchmarks, with one attaining the highest EWoK score among strict-track entries.

</details>


### [57] [An Analysis of Training-Free Self-Reported Confidence in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20541)
*Lukas Meyer,Sofia Rossi,Wei Chen,Thomas Laurent,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型自我报告置信度的可靠性，对比了直接口头化置信度、事后P(True)和多样本一致性三种无训练信号。实验发现直接口头化置信度是出人意料的强基线（AUROC 0.956/0.937），而三样本一致性较弱（0.765/0.790）且插值融合无显著提升。自一致性可能放大共享误解，置信度重提取会导致分数波动（0.043-0.084）和决策翻转（4%-9%）。传记声明审计显示置信度对真假声明区分度有限。结论指出自我报告对提示敏感、易受相关错误和基准噪声影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常伴随生成内容输出数值置信度，但不清楚这种报告是否仅是校准后的修辞。需要系统评估不同无训练置信度信号的校准质量，理解自我报告置信度的可靠性边界及其对提示、相关错误和基准噪声的敏感性。

Method: 在两个模型家族上，针对100个TriviaQA问题，分析三种无训练信号：1) 答案伴随的直接口头化置信度；2) 事后P(True)估计；3) 同一问题额外生成三个样本的一致性（三样本一致性）。评估指标为正确性预测的AUROC。审计基准错误，测试固定插值融合效果，考察重提取置信度的稳定性，并探索性审计100个带置信度标签的传记声明。

Result: 直接口头化置信度达到AUROC 0.956和0.937，显著优于三样本一致性（0.765和0.790）。固定插值融合无统计学显著收益。一个模型9个错误中4个、另一个模型8个错误中2个获得全票支持，表明自一致性放大共享误解。相同答案用等价提示重提取置信度，平均分数变化0.043-0.084，在0.8阈值下4%-9%决策翻转。传记声明审计显示真假声明置信度差距微小。

Conclusion: 有用的自我报告置信度仍高度依赖提示方式，易受相关错误和基准噪声影响。直接口头化是强基线但不稳定；自一致性可能强化共同谬误；置信度校准在实际知识声明上泛化有限。未来工作需改进提示鲁棒性、错误相关性建模和基准质量。

Abstract: Large language models can report a numerical confidence together with generated content, but it is unclear whether this report is more than calibrated rhetoric. We analyze three training-free signals: confidence verbalized with the answer, post-hoc $P(\mathrm{True})$, and agreement with three additional generations on the same 100 TriviaQA questions for two model families. Direct verbalization is a surprisingly strong baseline: after auditing benchmark errors, it reaches AUROC 0.956 and 0.937 for correctness prediction. Three-sample agreement is substantially weaker (0.765 and 0.790), and a fixed interpolation with verbalized confidence has no statistically reliable benefit. Four of nine errors from one model and two of eight from the other receive unanimous sample support, showing that self-consistency can amplify shared misconceptions. Re-eliciting confidence for the same fixed answers with equivalent prompts changes scores by 0.043 to 0.084 on average and flips 4\% to 9\% of decisions at a 0.8 threshold. An exploratory audit of 100 confidence-tagged biography claims further finds only a modest confidence gap between supported and contradicted claims. These results argue that useful self-reports remain sensitive to elicitation, correlated errors, and benchmark noise.

</details>


### [58] [Steering the Compass: Aligning Dynamic Psychological Counseling Conversations with Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20565)
*Zimu Wang,Yiwen Jiang,Xiangyu Zhao,Yaling Shen,Jiahe Liu,Stephanie Fong,Maxmartwell H Cheng,Guilherme C Oliveira,Anh Nguyen,Robert Desimone,Barnaby Nelson,Dominic Dwyer,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 StratCBT 数据集，包含 9,688 个心理咨询会话、约 25.6 万条对话轮次，每条咨询师回复均标注 8 种 CBT 策略之一。通过基于负性思维建模来访者，结合真实会话指导的自我对话生成高质量咨询对话，显著超越现有数据集。实验验证了策略对齐生成的有效性，并用 LLM 模拟来访者评估真实场景下的专业咨询效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究忽视了 CBT 依赖的动态决策过程，即根据来访者实时心理状态调整策略，导致灵活性和治疗效果受限。因此需要一个大规模、策略标注精细、且能反映真实咨询动态的数据集来推动 LLM 在 CBT 场景的应用。

Method: 1. 基于来访者负性思维构建客户画像；2. 引入真实咨询会话作为指导，采用自我对话机制生成高质量咨询对话；3. 将每条咨询师回复对齐到 8 种预定义 CBT 策略之一，构建 StratCBT 数据集；4. 训练并评估策略对齐的生成模型，并利用 LLM 模拟来访者进行真实场景效果测试。

Result: StratCBT 在通用咨询技能和 CBT 专项技能上均显著优于现有数据集。策略对齐生成模型能产出更专业、更有效的咨询回复，LLM 模拟来访者的评估进一步证实了其在真实场景下的治疗潜力。

Conclusion: StratCBT 填补了大规模、策略级标注、动态决策导向的 CBT 咨询数据集空白，为 LLM 驱动的心理咨询系统提供了高质量训练与评估基准，推动了自动化 CBT 向更专业、更个性化方向发展。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models have revolutionized the field of psychological counseling, especially in the context of Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT). While the success of CBT relies heavily on dynamic decision-making informed by the client's real-time mental state, this aspect has often been overlooked in current research, limiting both flexibility and therapeutic outcomes. In this paper, we introduce StratCBT, a dataset specifically designed for psychological counseling conversations with CBT Strategies, consisting of 9,688 sessions and around 256K utterances, with each counselor's response aligned with one of eight distinct strategies. The creation of StratCBT involves modeling clients based on their negative thoughts and generating high-quality counseling conversations through self-chat, incorporating realistic sessions as guidance, thereby significantly surpassing existing datasets in both general counseling and CBT-specific skills. We conduct extensive experiments to demonstrate the effectiveness of strategy-aligned generation and evaluate its efficacy in delivering professional and effective counseling with LLM-simulated clients to reflect real-world scenarios. The dataset can be obtained from https://github.com/zimuwangnlp/StratCBT.

</details>


### [59] [SAFARI: An Industrial Benchmark for LLM-Assisted Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20584)
*Chenxi Wu,Zimu Wang,Haiyang Zhang,Wei Wang,Zhijie Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFARI，首个工业级ISO 26262 HARA基准，包含3000个去标识化工业案例，评估九大前沿LLM在开放式危害分析与标准落地风险评估两大耦合任务上的表现。引入首个参考锚定的LLM-as-a-judge评测协议，专家相关性高。实验显示模型虽能生成看似合理的危害叙事，但在ASIL风险分类上极弱（最佳宏F1仅0.261），CoT提示收益有限且常降低分类性能。误差定位到危害生成时的场景关键上下文缺失与风险评估时的可控性误判，指出专家监督重点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在安全关键工程中的应用日益增多，但其在受监管的功能安全工作流（如ISO 26262 HARA）中的可靠性尚未充分探索。缺乏工业级基准与可靠评测协议，无法量化LLM在真实HARA任务上的能力边界与失效模式。

Method: 1. 构建SAFARI基准：收集3000个去标识化工业HARA案例，涵盖开放式危害分析与标准落地风险评估两大耦合任务。2. 提出首个参考锚定的LLM-as-a-judge评测协议，实现对开放式HARA产物的自动化评测，且与专家判断高度相关。3. 对九大前沿LLM进行系统实验，含零样本、少样本及Chain-of-Thought提示等设置。4. 开展细粒度误差分析，定位危害生成与风险评估两阶段的主要失因。

Result: 1. 模型能生成貌似合理的危害叙事，但在ISO 26262风险分类（ASIL）上表现极弱，最佳宏F1仅0.261。2. CoT提示对开放式生成收益有限，反而常降低分类任务性能。3. 误差主要源于：危害生成阶段遗漏场景关键上下文；风险评估阶段对可控性（Controllability）判断失误。4. 评测协议与专家判断高度相关，验证了自动化评测的有效性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM尚不具备在无专家监督下执行ISO 26262 HARA的能力。主要短板在于标准落地的分类推理与关键安全上下文的完整捕获。未来工作应聚焦于增强模型对功能安全标准的结构化理解、提升场景关键信息的保真生成，并将专家监督集中于可控性判断与上下文完整性校验等高风险环节。SAFARI基准与评测协议为后续研究提供了标准化测试床。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for safety-critical engineering, yet their reliability in regulated functional-safety workflows remains underexplored. We introduce SAFARI (Safety-Aware Functional Automotive Risk Inference), the first industrial benchmark for LLM-assisted automotive Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment (HARA) under ISO 26262. It contains 3,000 de-identified industrial HARA cases and evaluates two coupled tasks: open-ended hazard analysis and standards-grounded risk assessment. To evaluate open-ended HARA artifacts, we propose the first reference-anchored LLM-as-a-judge protocol with high expert correlation. Experiments with nine frontier LLMs show that models often produce plausible hazard narratives but remain weak at ISO 26262 risk classification, with the best ASIL macro-F1 reaching only 0.261. Chain-of-Thought prompting provides limited benefit and often degrades categorical risk assessment. Error analysis further localizes major failures to scenario-critical context omissions during hazard generation and to controllability misjudgments during risk assessment, indicating where expert oversight should be concentrated. The dataset can be obtained from https://github.com/xixi47520-hash/HARA.

</details>


### [60] [WiC is Not WSD: A Study on LLMs and Lexical Ambiguity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20593)
*Yi Zhou,Kiamehr Rezaee,Danushka Bollegala,Mohammad Taher Pilehvar,Jose Camacho-Collados*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究WiC任务中语言模型面临的挑战，假设缺乏显式义项库是原因之一，通过提供候选义项（类似传统WSD）提升WiC性能，并发现许多错误源于标签歧义或义项边界不匹配，而非词汇理解失败，LLM倾向于过度细粒度区分导致错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WiC任务对语言模型仍具挑战性，尽管词汇语义任务已有进展。作者假设困难不仅源于比较两个语境下的词用，还源于缺乏指定语义粒度的显式义项库。

Method: 在相似设置下评估开源LLM在WiC和传统WSD上的表现；提供候选义项（类似WSD）以改进WiC；进行人工评估以分析错误类型。

Result: 提供候选义项在所有设置下均提升WiC性能；显式义项信息帮助模型做出更一致、针对性的判断；人工评估显示许多表面错误实为标签歧义或模型与标注者义项边界不匹配；LLM倾向于过度思考义项区分，导致基于过度细粒度区分的错误。

Conclusion: WiC任务的困难部分源于缺乏显式义项库；引入候选义项可显著提升性能；模型错误多非词汇理解失败，而是标签歧义和过度细粒度区分导致；未来工作应考虑义项粒度对齐。

Abstract: Word-in-Context (WiC) remains challenging for language models, despite recent progress on lexical-semantic tasks. We hypothesise that this difficulty arises not only from comparing two contextual uses of a word, but also from the absence of an explicit sense inventory that specifies the relevant level of semantic granularity. We evaluate open LLMs on WiC and traditional Word Sense Disambiguation (WSD) under similar settings. We find that providing candidate senses, similar to what is done in traditional WSD, improves WiC performance in all settings. In general, explicit sense information helps models make more consistent and targeted judgements. Human evaluation further shows that many apparent WiC errors reflect label ambiguity or mismatches between model and annotator sense boundaries rather than simple failures of lexical understanding. In particular, results show that LLMs overthink the sense distinction often leading to errors based on overly fine-grained distinctions.

</details>


### [61] [What Does Privileged Information Add to On-Policy Self-Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20612)
*XiuYu Zhang,Wei Chow,Junfeng Fang,Zhenkai Liang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线策略自我蒸馏(OPSD)中参考答案的作用，构建了AMPLE-Math数据集，发现无参考蒸馏已占大部分性能提升，参考答案的额外价值主要在于跨模式迁移而非揭示解题过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是隔离并量化OPSD中教师模型获得额外信息（如答案或解题过程）对学生模型学习的具体贡献，区分蒸馏本身的效果与参考答案带来的增量收益。

Method: 构建AMPLE-Math数据集（5319道数学题，每题6种共享答案的推理视角），对比各视角与匹配的无参考蒸馏；在Qwen3-1.7B和SmolLM3-3B上实验，控制教师模式（思考启用/直接响应）、学生训练检查点、token级监督等变量。

Result: 无参考蒸馏在思考启用评估下占Qwen3-1.7B提升的大部分；参考答案额外收益温和，润色解答最强，完整轨迹在SmolLM3-3B步骤50仅增2个百分点；将短直接响应替换为长思考启用回滚会将增益变损失；改变token级监督基本不改变学生行为。

Conclusion: OPSD通过直接响应与思考启用推理共享的参数，改善对现有推理能力的访问；参考答案的价值在于其对跨模式迁移的补充，而非揭示了多少解题细节。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) lets a language model learn from a frozen copy of itself that sees an answer or a worked solution. Giving the teacher this extra information seems to offer the student more to learn, but how much does it add beyond distillation itself? To isolate that contribution, we construct AMPLE-Math, a reusable suite of 5,319 mathematical problems with six reasoning views that share the same answer, and compare each view with matched reference-free distillation. With a thinking-enabled teacher supervising direct-response rollouts, reference-free distillation accounts for much of Qwen3-1.7B's improvement under thinking-enabled evaluation, both in domain and on external benchmarks. Evidence for an additional reference benefit is modest in Qwen, strongest for a polished solution, whereas complete traces add two percentage points in SmolLM3-3B at step 50. These benefits depend on the student being trained. At the same checkpoint, replacing short direct-response rollouts with long thinking-enabled rollouts turns gains into losses in both families while the problems, references, and evaluation stay fixed. Teacher profiles and matched loss interventions in Qwen further show that changing token-level supervision can leave student behavior largely unchanged. Together, these findings suggest that OPSD can improve access to existing reasoning capabilities through parameters shared by direct-response and thinking-enabled inference. The value of a privileged reference is what it adds to this cross-mode transfer, not how much of the solution it reveals.

</details>


### [62] [Chronicle: Cut-Point Replay for Regression Testing of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20625)
*Tisha Chawla,Susheem Koul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Chronicle 是一种针对 LLM 智能体的记录-回放系统，它在非确定性边界（模型调用、工具交互）记录不可变信封，支持切点回放：从记录中回放选定边界，实时代码执行其余边界，将事故转为可在 CI 中运行的回归测试。实验显示记录开销极低（23 μs/次），全回放零模型调用且位稳定，切点测试能准确捕获故障代码并通过安全/良性变更，突变实验中捕获所有导致不安全动作的变异体，而全存根基线捕获零个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体的失败难以复现：模型输出非确定性、工具读取变化状态、多步轨迹极难重跑。现有记录-回放工具仅用于追踪或评分，无法针对代码变更进行回归测试。需要一种能将真实事故转化为可在持续集成中自动运行的回归测试的机制。

Method: Chronicle 在智能体运行的非确定性边界（模型推理、工具调用）插桩记录，生成不可变信封（envelope）序列。核心操作“切点回放”允许开发者选择一部分边界从记录回放，其余边界用新代码实时执行，形成针对特定事故的回归测试。系统提供完整回放（零模型调用、位稳定）与切点回放两种模式，并集成断言检查。

Result: 在 6 个模拟边界的记录失败基准上：记录开销 23 μs/次（占 300 ms 模型调用的 0.008%）；全回放零模型调用，20 次重复位稳定；切点测试在所有 6 个事故上对故障代码失败、对防护代码和良性变更通过。突变实验中，切点测试捕获所有放行记录中不安全动作的变异体，而同断言的全存根基线捕获 0 个。

Conclusion: Chronicle 通过轻量级边界记录与切点回放，实现了将 LLM 智能体事故转化为可靠、可在 CI 中运行的回归测试。其极低开销、位稳定回放与高精度故障检测能力，解决了智能体开发中缺乏可复现回归测试的关键问题，代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: Large language model responses are non-deterministic, so failures in LLM agents are hard to reproduce: a failure depends on inference that is not bitwise reproducible, on tools that read changing state, and on a multi-step trajectory that a re-run rarely repeats. Record-and-replay makes a run reproducible, but existing agent tooling records runs only to trace or score them, not to test a code change against them. We present Chronicle, which records an agent run at its non-deterministic boundaries as immutable envelopes and replays it from the record. Its central operation, cut-point replay, serves a chosen subset of boundaries from the record and executes the complementary subset live with new code, turning a recorded incident into a regression test that runs in continuous integration. On a benchmark of 6 recorded failures with simulated model boundaries, recording adds 23 μs per crossing (0.008% of an assumed 300 ms model call), full replay issues zero model calls and is bit-stable across 20 repetitions, and cut-point tests fail on faulty code and pass on guarded and benign changes for all 6 incidents. In a mutation study of the guarded tools, cut-point tests catch every mutant that lets the recorded unsafe action through, while a baseline that stubs every boundary, using the same assertion, catches none. Chronicle and the benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/theagentplane/chronicle.

</details>


### [63] [UniPolicy: Unified Objective-Specific Policies for Generative Search Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20630)
*Kun Yao,Yuhang Zhou,Yichi Zhang,Zeliang Tong,Shengri Xue,Haitao Wang,Siyu Lu,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 UniPolicy，一个面向搜索广告的目标感知多策略对齐框架。通过目标特定前缀 Token、稀疏 MoE-LoRA 路由和目标特定残差 FFN，在共享骨干网络中层级解耦参数，为相关性、点击倾向、商业价值等异构目标提供差异化策略表达空间。利用多阶段行为反馈构建成对偏好，增强点击样本的相对优势。推理时支持并行、业务可定制的多策略束搜索，在固定检索预算下灵活分配候选配额。大规模离线实验与 7 天线上 A/B 测试显示，CTR 提升 0.71%、RPS 提升 1.58%、广告收入提升 1.32%，且服务延迟稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索广告系统多采用单目标奖励（如 eCPM）或简单奖励融合对齐预训练生成模型，难以同时兼顾相关性、点击倾向、商业价值等异构目标，导致梯度竞争引发生成策略全局次优，无法在用户体验与商业价值间取得平衡。

Method: UniPolicy 包含三大核心设计：1) 层级解耦架构：目标特定前缀 Token 注入目标信息，稀疏 MoE-LoRA 路由按目标动态选择低秩适配器，目标特定残差 FFN 提供目标专属的前馈表达空间，三者在共享骨干上实现参数级解耦。2) 成对偏好构建：从多阶段行为反馈（曝光未点击、点击等）构建成对偏好，补充曝光未点击样本的相对偏好信息，强化点击候选在生成分布中的相对优势。3) 多策略并行束搜索：推理阶段并行运行多目标策略的束搜索，支持业务侧按目标自定义候选配额分配，满足固定检索预算下的灵活部署需求。

Result: 大规模离线实验表明，UniPolicy 在保持检索质量的前提下，在多指标上均衡提升，显著优于单目标强化学习与朴素奖励融合基线。线上 7 天 A/B 测试：CTR +0.71%，RPS（每搜索收入）+1.58%，广告收入 +1.32%，服务延迟保持稳定。

Conclusion: UniPolicy 通过层级参数解耦与成对偏好学习，有效解决了搜索广告中多异构目标的联合对齐难题，实现了用户体验与商业价值的双赢，且具备业务可定制的推理灵活性与工程落地的低延迟特性，验证了在真实大规模搜索广告系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: Search advertising connects user intent with commercial content and plays a critical role in platform monetization. Recent systems typically align pretrained generative models with a single business reward, such as eCPM, or use naive reward fusion for preliminary multi-objective alignment. However, an ideal search advertising system must jointly account for heterogeneous objectives, including relevance, click propensity, and commercial value, to balance user experience and business value while mitigating globally suboptimal performance caused by gradient competition. We propose UniPolicy, an objective-aware multi-policy alignment framework. UniPolicy combines objective-specific prefix tokens, sparse MoE-LoRA routing, and objective-specific residual FFNs to hierarchically decouple parameters within a shared backbone, providing differentiated parameter and policy-expression spaces for different business objectives. It further constructs pairwise preferences from multi-stage behavioral feedback, supplementing the relative preference information in exposed-but-unclicked samples and strengthening the relative advantage of clicked candidates in the generation distribution. At inference, UniPolicy supports parallel, business-customizable multi-policy beam search, flexibly allocating candidate quotas across objectives under a fixed retrieval budget. Large-scale offline experiments show that UniPolicy delivers balanced improvements across multiple metrics while preserving retrieval quality, outperforming single-objective reinforcement learning and naive reward-fusion baselines. In a 7-day online A/B test on a real search advertising system, UniPolicy improves CTR by 0.71%, RPS by 1.58%, and advertising revenue by 1.32%, while maintaining stable serving latency.

</details>


### [64] [HerHealthEval: Evaluating Multilingual and Register-Sensitive Understanding of Women's Health Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20684)
*Hassan Saeed Hassan Albattra,Mazen Mohammed Bahgat,Rahatara Ferdousi,Hana Essam Sayed Ahmed Amrya,Mariam Mousa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 HerHealthEval 多语言女性健康沟通评测框架，揭示聚合指标掩盖的安全风险，并通过语言不变风险标签重适配显著降低欠分诊率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型医疗评测多关注响应质量，忽视用户意图理解的多语言、多语域差异及不确定性处理，缺乏针对女性健康沟通的受控评测基准。

Method: 构建 HerHealthEval 基准，涵盖英、法、阿三语言及六种交际形式（规范、临床、外行、间接/委婉、情绪关切、故意欠规约），评估多语言指令模型及 QLoRA 适配变体在关注点分类、风险校准、澄清行为、解析合规、跨形式一致性等任务上的表现；引入源语言派生的语言不变风险标签进行受控重适配。

Result: 聚合准确率和一致性掩盖了安全相关失效；多语言适配模型在法语和阿拉伯语下欠分诊率高达 0.994；使用语言不变风险标签重适配后，欠分诊率分别降至 0.572 和 0.558。

Conclusion: 稳健的多语言医疗评测必须显式测试语域变体、不确定性处理及适配标签的来源与不变性，单纯依赖聚合指标不足以保障安全。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in healthcare communication, yet most evaluations emphasize response quality while assuming that the user's concern has been interpreted correctly. We introduce HerHealthEval, a controlled evaluation framework for multilingual understanding of women's-health communication. For each clinical case, HerHealthEval provides matched versions in English, French, and Modern Standard Arabic using six communicative forms: canonical, clinical, layperson, indirect or hedged, emotionally concerned, and deliberately under-specified. The first five express the same underlying concern and retain the same clinical information, whereas the under-specified form intentionally omits relevant details to test whether the model recognizes that clarification is needed. We evaluate a multilingual instruction model and QLoRA-adapted variants on concern classification, risk calibration, clarification behavior, parse compliance, and cross-form consistency. Results reveal that aggregate accuracy and consistency can conceal safety-relevant failures. A multilingual adaptation model reaches 0.994 under-triage in French and Arabic under language-asymmetric risk supervision. A controlled re-adaptation using source-derived, language-invariant risk labels reduces under-triage to 0.572 and 0.558, respectively. These findings show that robust multilingual healthcare evaluation requires explicit testing of register variation, uncertainty handling, and the provenance and invariance of adaptation labels.

</details>


### [65] [Summarization Bias: The Directional Collapse of Objective Projection into Told-Mode Labels in Large Language Models --- A Conceptual Framework and Registered Test Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20712)
*Levent Bulut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“摘要偏见”概念，指大语言模型（LLM）系统性倾向于用抽象摘要标签而非可重构的推理结构来表征叙事意义。在Bulut理论框架的“讲述-展示”轴上，LLM在生成和评估两种模式下均表现出偏向“讲述模式”的偏差：生成时默认显性宣告情感而非通过客观投射展示；评估时奖励显性陈述、低估“展示模式”的压抑手法。鉴于LLM越来越多地担任评判者和奖励模型，这种定向偏见可能施加选择压力，导致散文退化为平铺直叙的陈述。本文定义构念、关联现有LLM评判偏见、重读独立信度研究作为方向性证据，并预注册双模式检验及放弃标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在识别LLM在叙事生成与评估中沿“讲述-展示”轴的特定失效模式。由于LLM日益作为评判者和奖励模型被部署，若存在朝向“讲述模式”的定向偏见，将施加选择压力使散文质量下降，因此需形式化该构念并设计可证伪的检验。

Method: 理论构念定义：在Bulut理论框架内操作化“摘要偏见”，区分生成模式（默认显性宣告替代目标投射）与评估模式（奖励显性陈述、低估展示压抑）两种机制。文献定位：将该偏见置于LLM-as-judge偏见谱系中。证据重读：将既有独立信度研究重读为与该偏见一致的方向性证据。预注册实验：设计双模式检验方案并设定明确决策规则，若结果不支持则放弃构念。

Result: 未宣称偏见已获验证。完成了构念的形式化定义、理论定位、现有研究的方向性再解读，并产出可执行的预注册检验方案（含生成与评估两模式的决策规则）。

Conclusion: 摘要偏见作为一种假设性定向偏见，若成立将通过LLM评判机制对叙事质量产生系统性负面影响。本文提供了可证伪的理论框架与检验路径，为后续实证验证或构念修正奠定基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces and operationalizes summarization bias: a proposed systematic tendency of large language models (LLMs) to represent narrative meaning as an abstract summary label rather than as the reconstructable inferential structure that produces it. Within the Bulut Doctrine, narrative effect is theorized along a told-shown axis: in told mode, emotional and informational content is declared explicitly and requires little reader reconstruction; in shown mode, that content is suppressed at the surface and must be reconstructed from physical cues and indirection (Objective Projection). Shown mode is the higher-load condition the doctrine is designed to measure.
  The claim is that LLMs fail along this axis in a specific direction. Summarization bias is hypothesized to operate in two regimes: (i) a generative regime, in which a model asked to render an emotion through Objective Projection defaults to declaring it instead; and (ii) an evaluative regime, in which a model judging narrative quality rewards told-mode explicitness and under-detects shown-mode suppression. The evaluative regime is the more consequential, since LLMs increasingly serve as judges and reward models, and a directional bias toward told mode would impose a selection pressure degrading prose toward flat declaration.
  This report does not claim the bias is validated. It defines the construct, situates it against LLM-as-judge biases, rereads a completed independent reliability study as directional evidence consistent with it, and pre-registers a two-regime test with decision rules under which the construct would be abandoned.

</details>


### [66] [On-Demand Attention: Language Models Know When to Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20734)
*Haibo Feng,Ruiqi Liang,Hanyang Peng,Shiqi Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 On-Demand Attention (ODA)，一种局部优先的解码方法，通过轻量级回忆头预测全局注意力的收益并按需调用，仅训练回忆头保持预训练权重不变，在 vLLM 中实现 GPU 端条件执行，显著减少全局读取的同时恢复大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理和智能体工作负载对高效长上下文推理需求增加，但全注意力解码在每步都读取不断增长的历史，无论其对下一步预测是否有益，导致计算浪费。

Method: 利用预训练模型解码状态中已包含的预测收益信息，训练轻量级回忆头选择性调用全局注意力；仅训练回忆头，冻结预训练权重，保留完整历史 KV 缓存供未来回忆；在 vLLM 中实现 GPU 端条件执行将减少的全局读取转化为实际解码加速。

Result: 在 Qwen 和 Gemma 模型（含混合注意力骨干）上实验表明，选择性回忆在大幅减少全局读取的同时恢复了局部注意力下大部分损失的性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持这样的长上下文推理范式：预训练模型能够自主引导对其保留信息的访问，实现按需高效推理。

Abstract: Reasoning and agentic workloads increasingly demand efficient long-context inference. Yet full-attention decoding reads the growing history at every step, regardless of its benefit to the next prediction. We show that a pretrained model's decoding states already contain information predictive of this benefit, before the global read. Building on this finding, we introduce On-Demand Attention (ODA), a local-first decoding method that uses a lightweight recall head to selectively invoke global attention as its predicted benefit changes during generation. ODA trains only the recall head, leaving pretrained weights unchanged and the complete historical KV cache available for future recall. We further implement GPU-side conditional execution in vLLM, translating reduced global reads into practical decoding speedups over full attention at long context lengths. Experiments across Qwen and Gemma models, including hybrid-attention backbones, show that selective recall recovers most of the performance lost under local attention while substantially reducing global reads. These findings support long-context inference in which pretrained models guide their own access to the information they retain.

</details>


### [67] [dQwen3.5: Hybrid-Attention Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20751)
*Anton Xue,Litu Rout,Aditya Akella,Adam Klivans,Sujay Sanghavi,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究将混合架构（注意力与RNN交织）的预训练自回归模型Qwen3.5改造为扩散语言模型（DLM），发现混合骨干网络是高效的适配起点：与全注意力基线相比，仅需约一半的训练token即可达到相同损失，且在任意顺序解码和并行解码上表现强劲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归建模趋向混合架构（注意力+RNN），但RNN天然因果且难以双向化，这给扩散语言模型适配带来结构性障碍。作者想验证此类混合骨干能否成为有效的DLM起点。

Method: 在0.8B、2B、4B、9B四个规模上将Qwen3.5改造为扩散语言模型，得到dQwen3.5家族；与同规模全注意力Transformer对照，对比训练token效率、任意顺序解码行为及并行解码性能。

Result: 混合骨干仅需约一半训练token即达相同损失；各规模下dQwen3.5的任意顺序解码行为与全注意力DLM相似，并在并行解码中表现优异。

Conclusion: 混合注意力-RNN架构可作为高效的扩散语言模型适配基座，显著降低训练成本同时保持强解码能力，为大规模DLM训练提供实用路径。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained autoregressive (AR) model is a cost-efficient route to a diffusion language model (DLM). While nearly all such adaptations start from a full-attention transformer, AR modeling has shifted toward hybrid architectures that interleave attention and RNN layers. This creates an obstacle for adaptation: unlike attention, RNNs are structurally causal and nontrivial to bidirectionalize. Despite this mismatch, we investigate whether such backbones can become effective DLMs by adapting Qwen3.5 at 0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 9B scales, yielding the dQwen3.5 family. We find that hybrid backbones can be efficient starting points for adaptation: against a full-attention control, the hybrid reaches a given training loss in about half the tokens. Across scales, dQwen3.5 resembles full-attention DLMs in any-order decoding behavior and performs strongly under parallel decoding.

</details>


### [68] [Harm Laundering in GPT Models: Evidence That Gender Discrimination Is Transformed Rather Than Reduced Across Safety-Trained Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20779)
*Sarah Wyer,Sue Black,Noura Al Moubayed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示了大语言模型安全评估中存在的“危害洗白”现象：表面毒性分数下降，但显性歧视内容并未消除，而是转化为更隐蔽的表征性伤害。通过分析15个GPT系列模型的45万条性别导向生成，发现随着模型演进，针对女性的性暴力内容消失，但男性导向生成获得了积极表征（如照护、情感丰富、盟友身份），而女性导向生成未获同等提升；GPT-5甚至将乳腺癌框架化为男性权利辩论，且三大分类器均判定为无毒。情感极性在GPT-4反转：早期模型贬低女性，后期模型过度矫正。主题多样性在GPT-4对齐边界处女性导向生成较男性下降36%。REGARD表征伤害差异随发布时间显著增加（ρ=+0.55），而Detoxify毒性分数却下降（ρ=-0.23），表明毒性分数降低不能代表伤害减少。论文形式化了危害洗白的三准则测试，并提出三阶段检测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型安全评估主要依赖表面形式的毒性分类器，报告的危害分数随模型迭代持续下降，但这可能掩盖了歧视性内容的实质性转化而非消除。作者旨在揭示这种“危害洗白”现象，证明毒性分数下降不足以作为伤害减少的充分代理指标，并建立系统性的检测与评估框架。

Method: 1. 大规模生成收集：跨越GPT-2至GPT-5共15个模型，收集450,000条性别导向完成（女性导向、男性导向、中性三条件）。2. 主题建模与聚类分析：识别性暴力、照护、情感范围、盟友身份等语义簇，追踪其随模型演进的动态变化。3. 多维度伤害评估：使用三个独立毒性分类器、情感分析、REGARD表征伤害指标、Detoxify毒性分数、主题多样性指数等量化指标。4. 统计相关性检验：计算REGARD差异与模型发布日期的Spearman相关性（ρ=+0.55, p=.034）及Detoxify分数相关性（ρ=-0.23, p=.42）。5. 形式化定义与协议设计：提炼危害洗白的三准则（显性内容消失、隐性表征伤害持续/加剧、毒性分数虚假下降），设计三阶段检测协议（表面分类、表征审计、因果归因）。

Result: 1. 性暴力簇在GPT-2女性导向输出中显著，至GPT-4消失；男性导向输出在GPT-4/5获得照护、情感丰富、盟友身份等积极表征，女性导向输出未获同等增益。2. GPT-5主题5（1,997文档）将乳腺癌框架化为男性权利辩论，女性导向输出无对应簇，三大分类器均判定为无毒。3. 情感极性在GPT-4反转：早期模型贬低女性，后期模型过度矫正。4. 主题多样性在GPT-4对齐边界处女性导向较男性下降36%（W/M=0.58，GPT-2时为0.91）。5. REGARD表征伤害差异随发布日期显著正相关（ρ=+0.55, p=.034），Detoxify毒性分数负相关不显著（ρ=-0.23, p=.42），即毒性分数下降伴随表征伤害上升。6. 提出的危害洗白三准则测试与三阶段检测协议在OpenAI GPT谱系上得到验证。

Conclusion: 毒性分数的降低并非伤害减少的充分代理指标。大语言模型通过对齐训练实现了表面毒性的规避，但将显性歧视转化为更隐蔽、结构性的表征性伤害（危害洗白）。当前主流安全评估体系系统性地忽略了这种转化，需采用包含表征审计与因果归因的多阶段检测协议，才能真实评估模型的安全性。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for large language models rely on surface-form classifiers that report declining harm scores across model generations. We provide evidence that this methodology is systematically incomplete: explicit discriminatory content is transformed rather than removed. We call this \emph{harm laundering}. Analysing 450,000 gender-directed completions across 15 models spanning GPT-2 through to GPT-5 (OpenAI GPT lineage; three demographic conditions), we show that sexual violence clusters prevalent in GPT-2 women-directed output disappear by GPT-4, while men-directed completions gain positive representational territory (caregiving, emotional range, ally identity) that women-directed completions do not. The pattern is most visible at GPT-5: Topic~5 (1,997~documents) frames breast cancer as a men's rights debate, while zero equivalent clusters appear in women-directed output. Three independent classifiers score this content as non-toxic. Sentiment scores invert at GPT-4: early models demean women; later models over-correct. Topic diversity in women-directed completions falls 36\% relative to men at the GPT-4 alignment boundary (W/M~$= 0.58$, from $0.91$ at GPT-2). REGARD representational harm disparity correlates with release date ($ρ= +0.55$, $p = .034$) while Detoxify does not ($ρ= -0.23$, $p = .42$): toxicity scores fall as representational harm grows. We formalise harm laundering as a three-criteria test and provide a three-stage detection protocol applicable to any generative model. Within the OpenAI GPT lineage, toxicity score reduction is not a sufficient proxy for harm reduction.

</details>


### [69] [RetireOPD: Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20784)
*Yan Yu,Zhengxi Lu,Yizhou Liu,Yichen Pan,Aozhe Wang,Qipeng Chen,Hua Yang,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RetireOPD 是一种自退休在线策略蒸馏方法，通过自适应退休机制让无技能学生模型在与教师模型差异收敛且达到目标成功率比例后自主停止蒸馏，仅用 RL 继续训练，显著提升多轮智能体在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 任务上的表现，并超越教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 RL 的多轮智能体仅获得轨迹级稀疏奖励，自在线策略蒸馏 (OPD) 试图通过特权教师提供密集 token 级监督，但发现特权信息不一定保证教师可靠，且教师监督的收益随训练阶段变化，导致固定蒸馏调度效果不佳。

Method: RetireOPD 首先用环境奖励训练解耦的技能条件教师，再联合 RL 与 OPD 训练无技能学生。引入自适应退休：监控学生与教师的差异，当差异不再缩小且学生成功率达到教师的目标比例时，学生自动停止蒸馏，仅用 RL 继续训练。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5 1.5B 至 7B 模型上，RetireOPD 相较 RL 基线将 ALFWorld 成功率提升 14.1%–18.8%，WebShop 准确率提升 11.8%–19.0%，且在所有设置下均超越其技能条件教师。

Conclusion: 自适应退休机制有效解决了教师可靠性不足和阶段依赖性问题，使学生能在最优时机切换到纯 RL 训练，实现了比固定调度更高的样本效率和最终性能，为多轮智能体训练提供了更鲁棒的蒸馏范式。

Abstract: Multi-turn agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) receive a single scalar reward per trajectory, which motivates self on-policy distillation (OPD) to supply dense token-level supervision from a self-teacher with privileged task skills, letting a skill-free student internalize them. This recipe, however, is undermined by two findings in agentic tasks: privileged information alone does not always make a teacher reliable, and the benefit of teacher supervision is stage-dependent. We therefore propose RetireOPD (Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation), which first optimizes a decoupled, skill-conditioned teacher with environment rewards and then trains a skill-free student jointly with RL and OPD. Rather than following a predefined distillation schedule, RetireOPD adopts Adaptive Retirement: the student drops the teacher on its own once their discrepancy stops shrinking and it reaches a target fraction of the teacher's success rate, after which training proceeds with RL alone. Across Qwen2.5 models from 1.5B to 7B, RetireOPD improves ALFWorld success rate over RL baseline by 14.1% to 18.8% and WebShop accuracy by 11.8% to 19.0%, and surpasses its own skill-conditioned teacher in every setting.

</details>


### [70] [JEPA-Anything: Learning Predictive Models across Different Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20800)
*Taoyong Cui,Zhongyao Wang,Xinyue Xu,Weiyang Liu,Zhaochen Yu,Yuying Zhang,Qiang Gao,Mengyue Yang,Wanli Ouyang,Pheng Ann Heng,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA-Anything，一种基于正交预测因子分解(OPF)的领域无关框架，通过将潜在目标分解为互补因子、专用路径学习并重组，在七大领域(视觉、生物、临床轨迹、控制、分子动力学、物理场、天气)实现统一世界建模，超越匹配的JEPA基线并在干预预测、分子动力学长时程预测等任务上取得最优结果，且因子提名的生物干预获得实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型仍局限于特定领域，缺乏跨异构系统的通用学习原则。研究旨在探索是否存在一种通用的因子化预测原则，能够支持跨截然不同系统的世界建模，并将世界建模与干预预测及科学发现相连接。

Method: 提出正交预测因子分解(OPF)，扩展联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)：将潜在目标分解为互补因子，通过专用路径学习各因子，在共享预测设计中重组。构建JEPA-Anything框架，在七大领域统一评估，涵盖表征学习、干预预测、分布外泛化、长时程动力学等任务。

Result: 在10个匹配动力学任务上全面超越JEPA基线；干预乒乓球任务单干预预测误差降低34.8%；四个分子系统中一步和100步预测误差均最低；因子提名的生物干预在细胞共培养、患者来源类器官、肿瘤碎片和小鼠中获实验支持；潜在轨道模式恢复开普勒标度指数，拟合斜率-1.4991。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持跨异构世界存在通用的因子化预测原则，JEPA-Anything成功将世界建模与干预预测及实验验证的科学发现相连接，为通用世界模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: World modeling enables intelligence to anticipate consequences, guide interventions, and learn from interaction. Yet predictive models remain domain-specific: can a common learning principle support world modeling across radically different systems? We introduce JEPA-Anything, a domain-agnostic framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization (OPF). Extending joint-embedding predictive architectures, OPF decomposes latent targets into complementary factors, learns them through dedicated pathways, and recombines them within a shared predictive design. We evaluate JEPA-Anything across seven domains: vision, biology, clinical trajectories, control, molecular dynamics, physical fields, and weather. Experiments span representation learning, intervention prediction, out-of-distribution generalization, and long-horizon dynamics, including 10 matched dynamics tasks, forecasting of over 1,000 clinical events, and 100-step molecular rollouts across four systems. Against matched JEPA baselines, JEPA-Anything improves reported metrics on all 10 dynamics tasks and reduces single-intervention prediction error on Interventional Pong by 34.8%. It achieves the lowest one-step and 100-step molecular errors among compared methods in all four systems. Beyond prediction, a factor-nominated biological intervention receives experimental support in cell co-cultures, patient-derived organoids, tumor fragments, and mice; latent orbital modes recover the Keplerian scaling exponent with a fitted slope of -1.4991. These results support a common factorized predictive principle across heterogeneous worlds, connecting world modeling with intervention and experimentally grounded scientific discovery. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/JEPA-Anything

</details>


### [71] [Unifying Models of Intergroup Hostility in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20808)
*Patrick Gerard,Julia Mendelsohn,Kristina Lerman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将六大群体间敌意理论（边界建构、威胁建构、替罪羊机制、负面评价、非人化、行动导向）整合进统一实证框架，利用2024年美国大选期间TikTok、Truth Social和Twitter/X的286万条帖子，揭示了敌意修辞的结构锚点（边界建构与威胁建构）及时间演进规律（边界建构/贬损/行动导向早期出现，非人化/威胁建构中期，替罪羊机制最晚），弥合了社会科学传统的分裂，为计算社会科学建模群体间敌意修辞提供了超越单标签检测的实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对敌意言论助长排斥、两极分化与政治暴力的问题，现有社会心理学、道德心理学与政治科学的基础理论虽各自提出敌意生成机制，但长期平行发展、相互冲突且鲜少在真实话语中联合检验，导致对敌意修辞机制的理解碎片化，缺乏其在现实话语中如何呈现与关联的整体图景。

Method: 构建统一实证框架，对六大理论机制进行计算建模；采集2024年美国总统大选期间TikTok、Truth Social、Twitter/X三大平台共286万条帖子；从结构层面识别机制间的锚点关系，从时间层面分析机制在话语演进中的出现顺序与规律性。

Result: 结构上：边界建构与威胁建构构成敌意修辞系统的核心锚点。时间上：机制遵循稳定时序——边界建构、贬损（负面评价）、行动导向倾向早期出现；非人化与威胁建构出现较晚；替罪羊机制出现最晚。

Conclusion: 将理论框架映射至真实话语，跨越了社会科学传统的长期分歧，为计算社会科学建立起更清晰的实证基础，使群体间敌意修辞的建模能超越单一标签检测，走向机制层面的系统性理解。

Abstract: Hostile rhetoric toward social groups can normalize exclusion and justify mistreatment, as well as contribute to rising polarization and political violence. Efforts to moderate hostile rhetoric in online speech draw on foundational theories in social and moral psychology, and political science. However, these theories were developed largely in parallel, often propose different and sometimes conflicting accounts of how hostility develops, and have rarely been tested against each other in real discourse. The result is a fragmented understanding of the rhetorical mechanisms of hostility, without a clear sense of how they appear, and relate to each other, in real-world discourse. Using 2.86 million posts from TikTok, Truth Social, and Twitter/X during the 2024 U.S. presidential election, we model the mechanisms of six foundational theories of intergroup hostility -- boundary construction, threat construction, scapegoating, negative evaluation, dehumanization, and action orientation -- within a common empirical framework to recover the broader organization of intergroup hostility rhetoric. Structurally, we find that boundary construction and threat construction anchor the system; temporally, we find that these mechanisms tend to follow a regular ordering: boundary construction, derogation, and action orientation tend to appear early; dehumanization and threat construction later; scapegoating latest. Mapping how these theoretical frameworks actually manifest in discourse bridges longstanding divisions across social science traditions and presents computational social science with a clearer empirical foundation for modeling intergroup hostility rhetoric beyond single-label detection.

</details>


### [72] [Embedding Models Measure in Peculiar Ways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20821)
*Juri Opitz,Andrianos Michail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究词嵌入空间是否能反映质量、距离、时间、体积等物理测量的客观语义等价性与距离，发现嵌入空间仅弱建模物理测量，且表现出奇特的测量模式，受表层字符串相似性强烈影响，重新校准相似性难以显著改善对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理测量具有唯一、客观的语义等价与距离定义，适合作为基准评估嵌入空间捕捉真实世界语义结构的能力。现有研究多关注词汇语义相似性，缺乏对嵌入是否编码客观物理量的系统考察。

Method: 使用预训练词嵌入（如Word2Vec、GloVe、BERT等），构建物理测量词对（如'1 kg' vs '1000 g'），计算嵌入空间中的余弦相似度与欧氏距离，对比物理真实距离；分析字符串表面相似度（编辑距离、字符n-gram重叠）对嵌入相似度的影响；尝试通过重新缩放或正交变换重新校准相似度度量。

Result: 嵌入空间中物理等价词对（如'1 kg'/'1000 g'）的相似度显著低于表层形式相似词对（如'1 kg'/'1 kg'），物理量级距离与嵌入距离相关性弱；嵌入相似度主要由字符串表面相似度驱动，而非物理语义等价性；重新校准相似度度量（如线性变换、各向同性映射）无法显著提升物理测量的对齐质量。

Conclusion: 当前主流词嵌入未能可靠编码客观物理测量的语义结构，其几何特性主要反映训练语料中的表层共现与字符串模式，而非真实世界的度量关系。这揭示了嵌入空间在处理组合性、度量不变性等核心语义属性上的根本局限，提示需开发显式建模物理知识或符号推理的表示学习方法。

Abstract: Embedding spaces define notions of semantic similarity and distance. We study whether those embeddings reflect physical measurements of mass, distance, time and volume, which admit a unique, objective notion of semantic equivalence and distance. We find that physical measurement is only weakly modeled in the embedding space, and that instead quite peculiar measurement patterns can be observed. Further analysis indicates that embedding representations of physical measurements are strongly influenced by superficial string similarity, and recalibration of similarity does not substantially improve the alignment.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [73] [Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning: Stability under Constant Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19170)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Skylar Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了强调性时序差分学习（ETD）在常数步长下的采样动力学稳定性问题，构建了一个两状态反例证明ETD均值映射收缩但采样乘积具有正的顶部Lyapunov指数。提出了正则化强调性TD（RETD），通过泄漏标量状态存储ETD信号并释放延迟修正，保持轨迹和重要性比率不变。RETD的原始平衡点是ETD平衡点的仿射偏移，单/双正则化读出可精确恢复ETD不动点。证明了调和递减步长下的几乎必然收敛和基于马尔可夫随机乘积界的常数步长条件矩收缩结果。实验验证了RETD在两状态构造和Baird点上的负指数认证、不动点恢复、非单调稳定区域及任务依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ETD虽然稳定了期望的离策略TD更新并改变了投影几何，但这些性质都无法决定常数步长下的采样动力学。作者旨在解决ETD在常数步长下可能不稳定的根本问题，并提出一种在不改变轨迹和重要性比率的前提下修复后冲击动力学的算法。

Method: 1. 构建遍历两状态反例，通过再生周期分析将符号与后续轨迹的无限方差分离。2. 提出RETD：一种归一化的一阶后冲击修复，保持轨迹和重要性比率不变，将强调性TD信号存储在泄漏标量状态中，并释放延迟修正。3. 理论分析：证明调和递减步长下的几乎必然收敛，以及基于马尔可夫随机乘积界的常数步长条件矩收缩结果。4. 实验验证：配对10,000次运行实验，测试两状态构造、Baird点、不动点恢复、稳定区域和任务依赖性。

Result: RETD在两状态构造和一个Baird点上具有认证的负Lyapunov指数，而ETD在Baird点上仍为正指数（数值结果）。实验验证了：ETD与RETD在稳定性上的分离、RETD精确恢复ETD不动点、非单调稳定区域的存在、以及任务依赖性。RETD改变了后冲击动力学，但并未减少共享的后续轨迹方差。

Conclusion: RETD通过引入泄漏标量状态和延迟修正机制，成功解决了ETD在常数步长下的采样动力学不稳定问题，同时保持了ETD的不动点性质。该方法不依赖于减少后续轨迹方差，而是通过修改后冲击动力学实现稳定性。理论与实验双重验证了其有效性，为离策略强化学习的稳定性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Emphatic temporal-difference learning (ETD) stabilizes the expected off-policy TD update and changes its projection geometry, but neither property determines constant-stepsize sampled dynamics. We construct an ergodic two-state counterexample in which the ETD mean map contracts while the sampled product has a positive top Lyapunov exponent. Regenerative-cycle analysis separates this sign from the infinite variance of the follow-on trace. We introduce regularized emphatic TD (RETD), a normalized first-order post-shock repair that leaves the trace and importance ratios unchanged, stores the emphatic TD signal in a leaky scalar state, and releases a delayed correction. RETD's raw equilibrium is an affine shift of the ETD equilibrium; single- and two-regularization readouts recover the ETD fixed point exactly. We prove almost-sure convergence for harmonic diminishing stepsizes and a conditional constant-stepsize moment-contraction result from a Markovian random-product bound. RETD has certified negative exponents on the two-state construction and one Baird point, whereas the positive Baird ETD sign remains numerical. Paired 10,000-run experiments validate both separations, fixed-point recovery, a nonmonotone stability region, and task dependence. RETD changes post-shock dynamics; it does not reduce the shared follow-on-trace variance.

</details>


### [74] [BioPhys-Bridge: A Benchmark for Interdisciplinary Scientific Reasoning in Physics-Grounded Biological Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19180)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioPhys-Bridge是一个面向生物物理文献的证据驱动科学推理基准数据集，包含500个案例、1517个任务，覆盖六大生物领域和九大物理模型家族，用于评估模型的证据归因、推理忠实性、幻觉抑制及实验设计能力。初步评测显示DeepSeek-V4-Flash在证据ID F1上达0.360，领先于Qwen3.7-Max(0.316)和GPT-4o-mini(0.294)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在处理跨学科科学文献时面临独特挑战：生物物理研究要求将观测数据锚定于源证据，通过定量物理模型解释，并关联至生物学机制。现有基准缺乏对证据溯源、定量推理及多步科学推理的系统性评估框架。

Method: 构建BioPhys-Bridge基准，每个案例包含证据块、稳定证据ID、定量数值、单位、方程、假设、机制及下一步决策等 grounding targets。实施六重质量门控（模式校验、证据完整性、定量 grounding、源许可、去重、单位归一化），并邀请领域专家对81案例进行审查标注。首发版覆盖6生物域、9物理模型家族（含3稀疏家族预留扩展）。

Result: 初步评测中，DeepSeek-V4-Flash在证据ID检索任务上获得最高F1分数0.360，Qwen3.7-Max次之(0.316)，GPT-4o-mini为0.294。数据集及代码已开源于GitHub与Hugging Face。

Conclusion: BioPhys-Bridge为跨学科科学推理提供了严格的评估基准，支持对归因准确性、推理忠实性、幻觉减少及生物实验设计的量化考核。后续工作将扩充数据规模与复杂度，并开展全面系统的模型评测。

Abstract: Language models face unique challenges in analyzing interdisciplinary scientific research literature. In biophysics research, faithful answers require grounding observed data in source evidence, interpreting it through a quantitative physics model, and linking it to a biological mechanism. To address this challenge, we introduce BioPhys-Bridge, a novel benchmark dataset for evidence-grounded scientific reasoning over biophysical literature. Each case contains evidence blocks, stable evidence IDs, quantitative values, units, equations, assumptions, mechanisms, and next decisions as grounding targets for question answering (QA) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The initial release contains 500 cases, 1,517 agent-facing tasks, and covers six biological domains and nine physical model families, including three sparse families reserved for future expansion. We enforce strict quality gates for all cases in schema, evidence-integrity, quantitative-grounding, source-license, duplicate, unit-normalization, with domain expert review and annotation for 81 cases. Preliminary evaluations show that DeepSeek-V4-Flash obtain the highest evidence-ID $F_1$ score (0.360), followed by Qwen3.7-Max (0.316) and GPT-4o-mini (0.294). BioPhys-Bridge is an interdisciplinary benchmark for evaluating attribution, faithfulness, hallucination reduction, and biological experiment design with complex, multi-step scientific reasoning. Future works will increase the size and complexity of the dataset and perform comprehensive evaluations. Code and data are available in the GitHub repository and on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [75] [What Do We Expect from LLMs? Mapping the Design of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19182)
*Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了2022-2026年间arXiv上14,767篇大语言模型评测基准论文，揭示评测范式向动作、交互与专业应用转移，LLM自动评分普及但模型生成材料未同步增长，折射出评测建设与模型能力预期的共演关系，并警示评测扩展可能复制模型偏好与盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型排行榜难以反映评测需求的演变；基准的多样性变化折射研究者对LLM能力预期与成功标准的转移，需系统性量化分析评测资源的构建趋势。

Method: 对2022年1月至2026年8月arXiv提交的14,767篇引入或更新评测资源的论文，采用分阶段筛选与自动化全文编码，从目标系统与领域、评测材料与条件、计分机制三个维度追踪演变。

Result: 评测重心显著向行动、交互及专业应用倾斜，传统与新式设计要素并存；基于LLM的自动评分在智能体与非智能体评测中均持续增长，但模型生成评测材料在近期队列中未呈现同步持续增长。

Conclusion: 公开研究正将能力预期转化为具体测试与成功判准；随着AI参与造题、解题、评分，评测规模扩大是否提供更独立证据，还是复制参与模型的偏好与盲区，仍待审视。

Abstract: Benchmarks are central to how progress in large language models (LLMs) is assessed and communicated. Yet model rankings alone reveal little about how evaluation requirements themselves are changing. The expanding variety of benchmarks offers another perspective: what researchers expect LLMs to do, and what they count as successful performance. We systematically map 14,767 papers introducing or updating evaluation resources from arXiv submissions between January 2022 and August 2026. Using staged screening and automated full-text coding, we examine changes in target systems and domains, evaluation materials and conditions, and scoring mechanisms. The collection shows growing emphasis on action, interaction, and professional applications, while established and newer design elements frequently coexist. Model participation also develops unevenly: LLM-based scoring grows within both agent and non-agent groups, whereas model-generated materials show no comparable sustained increase in recent cohorts. These findings illuminate how public research translates capability expectations into concrete tests and criteria for success. As AI participates in constructing tests, performing tasks, and judging responses, they also raise a question: does expanding evaluation provide more independent evidence, or risk reproducing the preferences and blind spots of its participating models?

</details>


### [76] [Position: It is Time to Virtualize Foundation Models with a Self-evolving Operating System Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19203)
*Suparna Bhattacharya,Tarun Kumar,Cong Xu,Satish Kumar Mopur,Jiahao Li,Ashish Mishra,Aalap Tripathy,Annmary Justine Koomthanam,Martin Foltin,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了基础模型操作系统（FMOS）的概念，旨在解决当前复合智能体系统中运行时碎片化、行为不可移植、治理脆弱等问题，通过虚拟化基础模型交互实现类似操作系统的资源编排与策略自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 应用已从单一基础模型转向复合智能体系统，但现有技术栈碎片化严重：各框架隐式嵌入状态、记忆、预算、护栏等运行时，导致行为不可移植、治理困难，类似操作系统出现前每个程序重复实现基础服务的局面。

Method: 提出 FMOS 作为系统层，虚拟化基础模型交互（类比虚拟机抽象物理硬件），内部编排知识跨记忆层级、模型选择与资源分配、验证与策略执行；并引入类人脑的快慢思考机制，学习何时干预、何时直接推理，基于运行经验持续自适应调整策略。

Result: 论文为立场论文，未给出实验结果，但明确了 FMOS 的架构愿景：为应用提供专用、可信、能力近乎无界的基础模型实例假象，实现跨框架的可移植性与鲁棒治理。

Conclusion: 领域亟需基础模型操作系统（FMOS）作为统一系统层，通过虚拟化、编排与自适应策略解决复合智能体系统的碎片化难题，推动 AI 基础设施走向成熟、可治理的新阶段。

Abstract: AI applications have shifted from single, monolithic foundation models (FM) to compound agentic systems. Yet today's stacks remain fragmented: even as protocols (e.g., MCP, A2A) ease tool/agent connectivity, each framework embeds an implicit runtime for state, memory, budgets, and guardrails, making behavior non-portable and governance brittle. It mirrors computing before operating systems, when every program re-implemented basic services. This position paper argues that the field now needs a Foundation Model Operating System (FMOS) -- a system layer that virtualizes FM interactions analogous to how virtual machines abstract physical hardware, giving applications the illusion of dedicated, trustworthy FM instances with effectively unbounded capabilities. Internally, the FMOS orchestrates knowledge across memory tiers, model selection and resource allocation, and verification and policy enforcement. Like the human brain switching between fast intuition and slow deliberation, the FMOS learns when to intervene and when to let inference proceed directly and continuously adapting its policies based on operational experience.

</details>


### [77] [What Do Current Systematic Generalization Tasks Miss? A Reasoning-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19212)
*Chengwen Qi,Deheng Ye,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 TranSGrid 测试平台，统一演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理，系统评估 Transformer 的系统泛化能力。实验显示模型在 TranSGrid 上表现远差于常规测试集，且简化归纳或溯因需求会使任务退化为普通测试集，说明现有任务忽略了关键推理需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统泛化是人类智能核心，但现有研究依赖近似线性动作组合、基于生产力的测试、动作显式目标等简化，这些简化虽然便于研究，却遗漏了系统泛化的关键方面（归纳与溯因推理需求）。

Method: 构建 TranSGrid 测试平台，融合演绎、归纳、溯因推理于统一任务；在 4,800 个实例上测试七种 Transformer 模型；对比常规保留测试集与 TranSGrid 表现；引入两个变体分别降低归纳需求（近似线性动作组合）和溯因需求（动作显式目标），观察性能变化。

Result: 最大模型在保留测试集准确率 79.6%，TranSGrid 仅 55.3%，最难子集仅 15.8%；差距在训练长度范围内持续，说明生产力测试不足；两个简化变体均使解决率回升至接近测试集水平，表明任一简化都能将 TranSGrid 还原为普通测试集。

Conclusion: 现有任务要么降低归纳需求，要么降低溯因需求（或两者兼有），全面衡量系统泛化必须包含演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理的完整任务。

Abstract: Systematic generalization, the ability to solve novel problems by recombining known atomic elements, is central to human intelligence but difficult to study rigorously under controlled settings. Existing studies therefore rely on simplifications such as approximately linear action composition, productivity-based tests, and action-explicit goals, which make systematic generalization easier to study but omit some essential aspects of this capability. To characterize what these simplifications miss, we adopt a reasoning-centered lens and introduce TranSGrid, a testbed that brings deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning together within a unified task. Experiments with seven Transformers on 4,800 TranSGrid instances show that all models perform much worse on TranSGrid than on a held-out test set: the largest model solves 79.6% of the test set, but only 55.3% of TranSGrid and 15.8% of the hardest subset. The gap remains within the training length range, showing that productivity alone is not sufficient to evaluate systematic generalization. Additionally, we reintroduce the other two simplifications into TranSGrid: one variant makes actions compose almost linearly (reducing the inductive demand), the other makes goals action-explicit (reducing the abductive one). In both, solve rates return to roughly the test set level, showing that either simplification alone is enough to reduce TranSGrid to an ordinary held-out test set. Together, our results show that existing tasks reduce either or both of the inductive and abductive demands, and that comprehensively measuring systematic generalization requires a task that involves all three forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [78] [Characterizing Web Search by Conversational LLM Agents: From Search Decisions and Strategies to Results and Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19244)
*Mahsa Amani,Seungeon Lee,Abhisek Dash,Asmaa El Fraihi,Yunah Jang,Elisabeth Kirsten,Qinyuan Wu,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar,Soumi Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次跨四大对话平台(ChatGPT、Claude、Grok、DeepSeek)系统研究了Agentic Web搜索的全生命周期，结合真实用户交互(in-vivo)与受控API实验(in-vitro)，发现平台间搜索调用决策差异大、频繁调用不等于高质量、查询策略各异、搜索引擎存在域名偏好、且响应中存在未引用搜索结果的声称，引发归因与可靠性担忧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式LLM Agent越来越依赖Web搜索，但其端到端的Agentic搜索生命周期（何时调用、如何查询、结果偏好、如何生成）尚未被系统理解，亟需跨平台实证研究以指导未来AI Agent与对话式检索工具的设计。

Method: 采用混合方法：1) In-vivo：收集四大平台真实用户交互日志；2) In-vitro：使用同平台模型API进行受控实验，隔离模型行为；研究四个维度：搜索调用决策质量、查询重写策略、搜索结果域名分布偏好、搜索结果到响应的转换与引用行为。

Result: 1) 搜索调用决策在平台与模型间差异显著，高调用频率不保证响应质量更优；2) Agent采用多样化复杂查询重写策略（如分解、扩展、改写）；3) 平台专属搜索引擎返回结果呈现明显域名偏好（如自有生态、合作伙伴域名）；4) 响应大多基于搜索结果，但存在依赖未引用搜索结果的声称，引发归因缺失与可靠性风险。

Conclusion: 揭示了当前对话式Agentic搜索在决策一致性、查询策略透明度、搜索引擎中立性、引用完整性等方面的系统性短板，呼吁建立统一评估基准、透明化检索管线、强制引用机制，以构建更可靠、可审计的对话式检索系统。

Abstract: Conversational LLM agents increasingly rely on Web search, yet the end-to-end lifecycle of agentic search remains poorly understood. We present the first study of Web search across four major conversational platforms (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, and DeepSeek), combining real-world user interactions (invivo) with controlled experiments using the same platform's models by their APIs (invitro). We investigate the quality of agentic decisions to invoke Web search, their strategies to formulate queries, the potential domain preferences in the search results they receive, and the choices they make when transforming search results into grounded responses. We find that Web-search decisions vary substantially across platforms and models, while more frequent Web-search invocation does not necessarily yield better response quality. We further show that conversational agents employ different complex querying strategies and that platform specific search engines return search results from their preferred domains. Finally, although responses are largely grounded in search results, some claims rely on uncited search results, raising concerns about attribution and reliability. Our findings have important implications for the design of future AI agents and Web search tools optimized for conversational retrieval.

</details>


### [79] [Do AI Agents Understand Computer Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19387)
*Ambika Sharan,Grigory Chirkov,Soheil Abbasloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoTuring评估框架，通过对比同一智能体在具有语义意义的架构旋钮与匿名变量两种条件下的表现，量化架构知识对硬件设计智能体的真实贡献。实验发现：有意义的架构表示平均比盲搜索高出12.3%，比模拟H200高出5.4%，且仿真调用减少70.1%；但引入评论家循环后，盲智能体可弥补大部分差距，表明架构知识与结构化评论是替代而非互补关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件设计智能体评估无法区分智能体是真正理解架构原理进行推理，还是仅在参数空间中盲目搜索。传统评估固定问题框架、变换智能体，无法隔离“问题语义”这一变量。需要一种受控实验方法，单独测量架构语义知识对设计性能的边际贡献。

Method: AutoTuring框架：将相同的15维加速器设计空间以两种形式呈现给同一智能体——（1）具名架构旋钮+模拟器计数器（语义条件）；（2）[0,1]区间匿名变量（盲条件）。评估器、合法空间、可达最优解保持完全一致，唯一变量是问题表征是否包含架构语义。在9个FP16 GEMM内核组成的测试篮上，每条件运行5-6次，对比单一建模加速器上的表现。

Result: 语义条件下的架构师智能体平均超越建模H200 5.4%，超越盲智能体12.3%，仿真调用减少70.1%。引入评论家循环后，盲智能体恢复了大部分性能差距，而架构师智能体无进一步提升，表明架构知识与结构化评论为替代关系。结果为初步发现，基于单一加速器模型的少量运行。

Conclusion: AutoTuring确立了一种测量“问题意义”边际价值的方法论，而非优化特定加速器。核心贡献在于比较实验设计本身：证明架构语义显著提升样本效率与最终性能，但结构化评论机制可替代显式架构知识，二者为替代而非互补关系。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly asked to design hardware, and increasingly reported to succeed. Such reports establish that a design improved; they cannot establish why. An agent that improves an accelerator may be reasoning about the machine, or may be searching competently over knobs whose meaning it never recovers -- and only the first transfers to the next architecture. Existing evaluations cannot tell the two apart, because they vary the agent while holding the framing of the problem fixed. We do the opposite. AutoTuring hands the same agent the same 15-dimensional accelerator space twice: once as named architectural knobs with simulator counters, once as anonymous variables on [0,1], with the evaluator, the legal space and the reachable optima held identical, so that the only thing that varies is whether the problem means anything. The gap between the two is the measurement. On a nine-kernel FP16 GEMM basket, meaning pays: the architect beats a modeled H200 by 5.4% and its blind counterpart by 12.3% on average, with 70.1% fewer simulator calls. It does not pay uniquely: a critic loop recovers most of that gap for the blind agent and buys the architect nothing, so architectural knowledge and structured critique behave as substitutes rather than as complements. We report these as preliminary findings -- five to six runs per condition on a single modeled accelerator -- and take the comparison itself, not the accelerator, to be the contribution.

</details>


### [80] [MAGS: Multi-agent Auto-formalization Guarantees Safety for Agentic Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19391)
*Albert Wu,Nicholas Roberts,Tzu-Heng Huang,Haoran Lin,Gil Friedman,Sungjun Cho,Gabriel Orlanski,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MAGS多智能体框架，利用Dafny作为验证感知的中间表示，实现带有形式化安全保证的可执行程序自动生成。在CUDA内核、终端脚本、机械臂任务共220个案例上达到100%成功率，但揭示自动形式化语义未完全捕获目标行为时会出现失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体生成复杂程序的规模使得人工审查日益困难，增加安全风险。现有模糊测试、静态分析、LLM验证器等方法难以覆盖所有边缘情况。形式化验证虽能提供机器可检查保证，但传统上需要大量手动规范和证明工程。

Method: MAGS统一多智能体框架：冻结人工审核的API和安全需求，将生成代码翻译为Dafny，利用验证器反馈修复违规，将验证通过的程序编译回目标可执行代码。Dafny作为验证感知中间表示，实现安全属性的机械化检查。

Result: 在100个CUDA内核、100个终端脚本、20个机械臂任务共220个案例上，MAGS实现100%成功率生成符合冻结规范的非平凡安全保证程序。独立安全与功能评估显示三大领域均表现强劲，但自动形式化语义未完全捕获目标行为时会出现失败案例。

Conclusion: MAGS证明了将形式化验证集成到LLM代码生成流水线中是可行且有效的，能大规模生成具备机器可检查安全保证的程序。但自动形式化与真实语义的差距仍是关键挑战，需进一步研究语义对齐与规范完备性。

Abstract: LLM coding agents now generate complex programs at a scale that makes thorough human review increasingly difficult, raising the risk of safety and security failures. Common approaches, including fuzz testing, static analysis, and LLM-as-a-Verifier, can detect many failures but struggle to cover all possible edge cases. Formal verification addresses this by providing machine-checkable guarantees over specified properties, but traditionally demands substantial manual specification and proof engineering. We introduce a unified multi-agent framework, MAGS, that generates executable programs with formal safety guarantees, using Dafny as a verification-aware intermediate representation where safety properties can be mechanically checked. MAGS formalizes and freezes human-audited APIs and safety requirements, translates generated code into Dafny, repairs violations using verifier feedback, and compiles verified programs back into executable code. We evaluate MAGS on 100 CUDA kernels, 100 terminal scripts, and 20 robotic-arm tasks. Across all 220 examples, it achieves a 100% success rate in producing programs with non-trivial safety guarantees against frozen specifications. Independent safety and functional evaluations further show strong performance across all three domains, while revealing failures when the auto-formalized semantics do not fully capture the target behavior.

</details>


### [81] [Closed-World Resolution Against Tool Hallucination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19425)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究工具增强型大语言模型（LLM）代理中的“工具幻觉”问题，提出五类工具幻觉分类法（H1-H5）及基准测试 HTB，证明幻觉防御必须先于任何因果门控，并在单注册表与多服务器合并（MCP）两种调用面上实测了 322 与 154 例真实幻觉，发现模型规模不降低幻觉率，且命名空间合并会引入结构性冲突与遮蔽导致新型幻觉（M1-M5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择与工具安全机制均预设模型调用的工具真实存在，但模型会凭空捏造工具名或传递未声明参数，这种幻觉调用根本不在任何门控的决策空间内，因此无法被现有防御拦截；论文旨在量化这一结构性盲区，并建立可比较的基准。

Method: 1. 定义五类工具幻觉分类法（H1-H5）与参考基线 Resolution Rung（无训练、闭世界解析器：注册表成员资格检查 + 签名校验）。2. 在十款托管模型上，对原始 JSON 与结构化调用两种调用面测量 322 例真实幻觉。3. 扩展至 Model Context Protocol（MCP），定义第二分类法（M1-M5）捕捉多服务器合并命名空间产生的冲突与遮蔽幻觉，在实时 MCP 面测量 154 例幻觉。4. 发布版本化 Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark（HTB）供统一评测。

Result: • 原始 JSON 面捏造工具调用 34 例，结构化面仅 3 例，幻觉高度集中于非约束面。• 模型规模无帮助：675B 模型幻觉率与 7-8B 模型相当。• 单注册表面“干净”的前沿模型，在 MCP 合并命名空间下仍产生幻觉，因冲突与遮蔽是合并的结构性属性。• 证明幻觉防御必须前置于任何因果门控，并刻画出唯一不可消除的残留（借用参数且与合法调用 schema 不可区分）。

Conclusion: 工具幻觉是独立于工具选择与门控的正交风险，必须在调用进入执行管线前通过闭世界解析器拦截；模型扩大规模无法缓解，多服务器命名空间合并会引入本质上不可通过单一注册表消除的新型幻觉面。HTB 基准为后续解析器/防御机制提供了可复现的对比标准。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents fail in a way no tool-selection or tool-security method addresses: they call tools that do not exist and pass arguments no schema declares. Existing defenses either pick the right tool (selection) or constrain what an agent may do with real tools (gating), both of which presuppose the emitted call refers to a real tool at all. We show this is a structural blind spot: a hallucinated call is by construction not a decision any gate made, so no gate can reject it. This paper is primarily a measurement and benchmark study. We give a five-class taxonomy of tool hallucination (H1-H5) and, as a reference point, the Resolution Rung: a training-free, closed-world resolver (registry membership plus a signature check) whose interest is where it must sit, not what it computes. We prove hallucination defense must precede any causal gate, and characterize the one irreducible residue (borrowed arguments schema-indistinguishable from a valid call). Across ten hosted models under two invocation surfaces we measure 322 genuine hallucinations; fabricated-tool calls concentrate on the unconstrained raw-JSON surface (34 vs. 3), and model scale does not help (a 675B model matches a 7-8B one). We then extend to the Model Context Protocol, where merging several servers into one namespace creates hallucination surfaces a single registry cannot express (a second taxonomy, M1-M5); on the live MCP surface we measure 154 hallucinations, including from frontier models that were clean on the single-registry surface, because collisions and shadowing are structural to the merge. We release the versioned Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB) so any resolver is comparable across submissions.

</details>


### [82] [The syntax and semantics of goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19448)
*David M. Abel,Mark K. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将目标视为认知表征，探讨其表达能力、设计与效率，综合相关研究提出目标表征的设计空间，并主张区分目标的形式与意义以阐明隐含假设、促进高层认知与动机交互研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学与计算机科学中目标常被视为固定约束行为的认知状态，但缺乏对目标表征本身性质（如表达力、等价性、效率）的系统分析。文章借鉴语言学语法-语义接口视角，指出需从表征层面审视目标，以解决目标表征的基础理论问题。

Method: 采用理论综合与概念分析方法，整合认知科学、计算机科学及语言学中关于目标表征的研究，从表征形式与内容的区分出发，构建目标表征的设计空间框架，表征不同目标语言在表达力、约束条件等维度的权衡。

Result: 指出目标表征可作为设计空间中的点，不同表征在表达力、计算效率、行为捕获能力等方面存在权衡；区分目标的形式（句法结构）与意义（语义内容）可揭示隐含假设、指导高层认知与动机交互研究、分离目标概念的变异维度。

Conclusion: 将目标视为表征并区分其形式与意义，有助于统一认知科学与计算机科学中的目标理论，为未来研究提供分析框架，推动对目标表征的表达力、设计原则及认知机制的深入理解。

Abstract: In both cognitive science and computer science, goals are conceptualized as cognitive states that flexibly combine with world knowledge to organize and specify purposeful behavior. In this way, goals are compositional representations whose content relates to rational behavior. We here draw attention to goals as representations and their content because it highlights a parallel with other areas in cognitive science - in particular, the syntax-semantics interface in linguistics and logic - while also foregrounding foundational questions about the expressivity, design, and efficiency of different goal representations. For example, goals are typically taken as fixed and imposing constraints on desirable behaviors, but we can also identify constraints on goal representations themselves, such as whether a particular goal language is sufficiently expressive to capture behaviors of interest, or whether different goal representations capture the same behavior. Here, we synthesize work that aims to characterize the properties of different goal representations and suggest these are points of a broader design space. We close by discussing how distinguishing the form and meaning of goals can elucidate the implicit assumptions we make about goals, inform the study of interactions between higher-level cognition and motivation, and isolate axes of variation for different conceptions of goals.

</details>


### [83] [Compositional Reasoning in Language Models under Reinforcement Learning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19465)
*Yu He,Yingxi Li,Yifei Wang,Ellen Vitercik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出依赖图框架形式化组合推理，发现分解技能训练难以迁移到组合任务，而组合任务训练更易迁移回分解任务，并提供理论解释，初步验证在工具调用基准上的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合推理对现实问题求解至关重要，但训练后方法（如强化学习）对组合推理的影响尚不明确，需要系统研究模型如何通过组合已学技能实现泛化。

Method: 提出依赖图框架定义三个复杂度递增的组合性层级，以数据结构任务实例化（确定性奖励、清晰组合结构），实证研究分解技能与组合任务的双向迁移，并提供理论分析；另在工具调用基准上进行试点研究。

Result: 发现一致的分解到组合不对称性：分解技能训练不可靠地迁移到组合任务，而组合任务训练更易迁移回分解任务；在长度外推、结构分布偏移、未见技能迁移等设置下验证组合泛化；工具调用基准显示不对称性可能延伸至实际场景。

Conclusion: 组合推理存在根本的分解-组合不对称性，组合任务训练能更好地促进技能组合泛化，该发现对后训练方法设计有重要启示，且初步证据表明该现象适用于实际工具调用任务。

Abstract: Compositional reasoning is critical for real-world problem solving: since training data is necessarily limited, models must generalize by composing learned skills in new ways. While post-training methods such as reinforcement learning (RL) have substantially improved the reasoning abilities of language models (LMs), their effects on compositional reasoning remain less well understood. We propose a dependency-graph framework to formalize compositional reasoning, yielding three levels of compositionality with increasing complexity. Empirically, we instantiate this framework with data-structure tasks, which provide deterministic reward computation and clear compositional structure. We find a consistent decomposed-to-composed asymmetry: decomposed-skill training does not reliably transfer to composed tasks, whereas composed-task training transfers more readily back to decomposed tasks. We provide theoretical explanation for this asymmetry, and further evaluate compositional generalization under length extrapolation, structural distribution shift, and transfer to tasks requiring unseen skills.
