The architecture is pretrained on five public predictive maintenance datasets (CWRU, MFPT, UOC18, PRONOSTIA, CMAPSS) using masked auto-encoding and contrastive learning with balanced domain sampling. On fault diagnosis, the model achieves 99.2% accuracy on CWRU (+6.4 pp over CNN) and 82.1% zero-shot accuracy on MFPT, demonstrating strong transfer across sampling frequencies. However, the approach does not improve remaining useful life prediction, suggesting a mismatch between pretraining and RUL objectives that warrants future investigation.

</details>


### [153] [Refuse, Decompose, Refresh: A Claim-Safe Protocol for Closed-Loop AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20538)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“claim-safe”评估协议，旨在解决闭环系统中AI评估可复现但支持错误声明的风险。协议包含三个动作：拒绝、分解、刷新。在包含24个策略组件、三种需求制度、两类故障掩码的聚合仿真器中实例化，预注册保留集含1,440个案例和21,600个分区行。结果显示仅55/72个制度-组件单元通过参考准入，54/55通过运行时准入，稳定虚假准入为0/20，上界0.1391；受影响洁净流量优于名义故障单元分数；漂移日志表明“空值”必须相对于参考定义。贡献是一个可执行契约，关联可观测支持、统计校准与合理声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估虽可完美复现，仍可能支持错误声明，尤其在闭环系统中：策略决定访问状态、可观测组件及哪些故障留下可测痕迹。现有评估缺乏将可观测支持、统计校准与合理声明相联系的机制，导致评估结果可能被误读或过度推广。

Method: 提出claim-safe协议，包含三个动作：1) Refuse（拒绝）：当缺乏洁净参考流或匹配的运行时比较支持时弃权；2) Decompose（分解）：将协议执行、操作性虚假准入、结构性假设分开报告，而非合并为单一PASS/FAIL标签；3) Refresh（刷新）：将分布偏移报警视为使参考图失效并重新计算的请求，而非故障证据。在聚合仿真器中实例化，含24策略组件、3需求制度、2故障掩码族，独立开发与保留种子。预注册保留集含1,440案例、21,600分区行。

Result: 55/72制度-组件单元通过参考准入，54/55通过运行时准入，弃权成为结果一部分。稳定虚假准入0/20表示组件，单侧精确95%上界0.1391（冻结0.20规则）。准入单元内，受影响洁净流量优于名义故障单元分数：540单元-臂行嵌套于20组件簇，单元减流量负对数似然差0.1264 nats/行，95%组件簇区间[0.0593, 0.1918]。漂移日志显示洁净故障空值流在三制度触发15/15、0/15、14/15报警，仅中间制度匹配冻结检测器参考，证明“空值”必须参考相对。

Conclusion: 论文贡献一个可执行契约，将可观测支持、统计校准与合理声明相联系，而非提供通用阈值。Claim-safe协议通过拒绝、分解、刷新三动作，使评估结果更诚实反映证据强度，避免错误声明。实验证明协议能量化准入率、虚假准入界、预测优势及参考相对性，为闭环系统评估提供严谨框架。

Abstract: An AI evaluation can be perfectly reproducible and still support the wrong claim. This risk is acute in closed-loop systems: policy determines visited states, observable components, and which failures leave a measurable trace. We propose a claim-safe protocol with three actions. Refuse: abstain when a clean reference stream or matched runtime comparison lacks support. Decompose: report protocol execution, operational false admission, and structural hypotheses separately rather than as one PASS/FAIL label. Refresh: treat distribution-shift alarms as requests to invalidate and recompute a reference map, not as fault evidence. We instantiate the protocol in an aggregate-only simulator with 24 policy components, three demand regimes, two fault-mask families, and independent development and heldout seeds. The preregistered heldout contains 1,440 cases and 21,600 partition rows. Only 55/72 regime-component units were reference-admitted and 54/55 remained runtime-admitted, making abstention part of the result. Stable false admission was 0/20 represented components, with a one-sided exact 95% upper bound of 0.1391 under a frozen 0.20 rule. Within admitted units, affected clean traffic outpredicted nominal fault-cell fraction: across 540 unit-arm rows nested in 20 component clusters, the cell-minus-traffic negative-log-likelihood difference was 0.1264 nats per row, with a 95% component-cluster interval of [0.0593, 0.1918]. A drift log shows why "null" must be reference-relative: clean fault-null streams triggered 15/15, 0/15, and 14/15 alarms across three regimes, while only the middle regime matched the frozen detector reference. Rather than a universal threshold, we contribute an executable contract linking observable support, statistical calibration, and justified claims.

</details>


### [154] [Language-model groups overstate consensus when replaying human deliberation on a reasoning task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20543)
*Tengfei Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过将100个人类Wason任务小组与匹配的LLM智能体小组进行对比，发现智能体小组表现出显著更高的全共识率（相差34-44个百分点），且这种差距在不同测量方式下保持稳定。模拟共识并不追踪集体准确性，信念锚定智能体小组是人类小组结果分布的有偏估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全共识率常被视为集体认知的指标，但其取决于参与度和最终状态的操作化定义。现有研究缺乏对人类与LLM智能体小组在相同评分标准下的系统对比，需要明确评分标准来评估模拟小组对人类审议结果的估计偏差。

Method: 重放100个保留的人类Wason小组实验，构建匹配的LLM智能体小组，每个参与者讨论前的答案作为一个信念锚定智能体的种子。使用相同代码对智能体和人类进行评分。进行两种事后解盲敏感性分析：提交基础比较（n=98）和参与度匹配比较（n=45），分别考察聊天模式和推理模式。

Result: 人类评分定义下的全共识率估计范围为24.0%-57.0%，约五分之一参与者从未发帖，而智能体几乎总是参与。智能体小组在两种敏感性分析中均显著更具共识性：提交基础比较中聊天模式差距34.0个百分点，推理模式43.9个百分点；参与度匹配比较中分别为34.1和44.4个百分点。两种互补路径减少了不同测量不对称性，却在0.5个百分点内收敛。差距在无早停和移除可记忆答案的重新参数化下持续存在；推理模式小组几乎一致同意，但多为错误答案。模拟共识不追踪集体准确性，信念锚定智能体小组是人类小组结果分布的有偏估计器。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个明确评分标准的基础，用于评估模拟小组对人类审议结果的估计。结果表明，LLM智能体小组系统性高估共识率，且共识不代表准确性，信念锚定方法存在系统性偏差，需谨慎使用模拟小组推断人类集体认知过程。

Abstract: Full-consensus rates are often treated as indicators of collective cognition, yet depend on how participation and final states are operationalized. We replayed 100 held-out human Wason groups with matched large language model (LLM) agent groups, seeding one belief-anchored agent per participant's pre-discussion answer and scoring agents and people with the same code. Across human scoring definitions, estimates ranged from 24.0% to 57.0%; about one fifth of participants never posted, whereas agents almost always did. Agent groups remained more consensual in two post-unblinding sensitivity analyses: the submit-based comparison (n = 98) yielded gaps of 34.0 and 43.9 percentage points for chat and reasoning modes, and the participation-matched comparison (n = 45) yielded gaps of 34.1 and 44.4 points. These complementary routes reduced different measurement asymmetries yet converged within 0.5 percentage points. The gap persisted without early stopping and under a reparameterization removing the memorizable answer; reasoning-mode groups then agreed nearly unanimously, mostly on incorrect answers. Simulated consensus did not track collective accuracy, and belief-anchored agent groups were biased estimators of the human group-outcome distribution in this setting. These analyses provide a scoring-explicit basis for assessing simulated-group estimates of human deliberative outcomes.

</details>


### [155] [Limits of Confidence in Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20581)
*Russ Webb,Amitis Shidani,Alice Bizeul,Dan Busbridge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出离散扩散模型（如重掩码和均匀状态采样器）在每步写入多个token位置时，若这些位置存在条件依赖，则无法匹配训练分布；通过合成任务ScanAndAdd验证了多位置写入的依赖性，并量化了生成分布与采样噪声底噪的总变分差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离散扩散模型在生成序列时，多token位置并行写入的理论局限性，揭示逐位置分布无法捕捉token间的固有依赖关系，从而解释生成质量下降的根本原因。

Method: 理论分析：证明步骤匹配训练分布的充要条件是写入位置在已固定token条件下独立；实验验证：在合成任务ScanAndAdd（联合分布可闭式求解）上，检验置信度排序写入的每组未确定位置的依赖性，测量生成分布的总变分距离与采样噪声底噪的比值。

Result: 理论上证明：任意逐位置分布的乘积均无法匹配依赖组，且逐位置边缘分布不决定群体依赖性。实验上：ScanAndAdd任务中，每组两个或以上未确定位置均存在依赖，生成分布总变分距离为采样噪声底噪的29倍，但逐样本指标为1.0（即表面指标正常，实则分布偏差大）。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的并行写入机制因忽略token间条件依赖，导致生成分布偏离真实分布；需开发能建模位置间依赖的采样策略或架构改进，以缩小分布差距。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion, including remasking and uniform-state samplers, generate a sequence by writing multiple token positions per step, drawing each from a per-position distribution and choosing which positions to write from those same distributions. For domains of general interest (pixels, phonemes, or words) there are inherent dependencies between tokens. We show that a step matches the training distribution only when the positions it writes are conditionally independent given the tokens already fixed, that no product of per-position distributions can match a dependent group, and that per-position distributions do not determine whether a group is dependent: two joint distributions can have identical per-position marginals while differing in which combinations of values occur. On ScanAndAdd, a synthetic task whose joint distribution is available in closed form, we verify that every group of two or more undetermined positions a confidence ranking writes is dependent, and measure the generated distribution to be $29\times$ the sampling-noise floor total variation while per-sample metrics are $1.0$.

</details>


### [156] [PAA: The Probabilistic Allen Algebra: A Generative and Complete Probabilistic Extension of Allen's Interval Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20634)
*Julian Eggert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率 Allen 代数 (PAA)，一种生成式且完备的 Allen 区间代数扩展，通过区间边界上的概率分布（高斯时间点、高斯中点与截断高斯时长）自然推导 13 种基本关系的概率，解决了原始代数无法处理不确定时间信息的问题，并提供开源 Python 实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Allen 区间代数的 13 种基本关系是建立在精确区间边界之上的 crisp 谓词，无法处理来自自然语言、感知、数据库或不确定历史中的模糊时间信息（如“刚刚之前”、“大致期间”等分级表达），因此需要一种能从边界分布生成关系概率、支持不确定性建模且保持代数完备性的概率扩展。

Method: 将时间点建模为高斯分布，区间建模为高斯中点与截断高斯时长；在同一概率空间中，所有关系均表示为边界排序谓词：点-点关系归约为误差函数，点-区间与区间-区间关系归约为由线性不等式诱导的多元高斯正交象限概率；引入容差带使接触关系获得正测度，在单一容差下 13 种关系构成真划分，容差趋零时退化为 crisp Allen 代数；该构造自下而上推导 Allen 分类体系，粗粒度谓词为叶节点并集，概率为叶节点概率之和，层级在区间坍缩为点时自动保持。

Result: PAA 具有尺度不变性，能将“稍早于”等分级表达与接触关系分离；所有理论结果经 Monte Carlo 仿真验证，并作为开源、经过测试的 Python 包发布。

Conclusion: PAA 为不确定时间推理提供了一个理论完备、计算可行且工程可用的概率框架，统一了从精确到模糊、从点到区间、从基本关系到粗粒度分类的各层级语义，填补了定性时序推理与概率建模之间的空白。

Abstract: Allen's interval algebra is a qualitative calculus for temporal relations, but its thirteen base relations are crisp predicates over exact interval boundaries. This is inadequate for temporal information from language, perception, databases, or uncertain histories, where times, durations, and boundaries are uncertain and expressions such as "just before" or "roughly during" have graded meaning. We develop the probabilistic Allen algebra (PAA): a generative and complete extension in which relation probabilities are derived from distributions over interval boundaries rather than assigned as scores. Time points are Gaussian; intervals have Gaussian midpoints and truncated-Gaussian durations. Every relation is a boundary-ordering predicate in one common probability space: point-point relations reduce to error functions, and point-interval and interval-interval relations to multivariate Gaussian orthant probabilities induced by linear inequalities. Contact relations (meets, starts, finishes, equals) receive positive measure through a tolerance band, and under a single tolerance the thirteen relations form a true partition that recovers crisp Allen as the tolerance vanishes. The construction derives Allen's taxonomy rather than positing it: coarse predicates such as precedence, overlap, and containment are unions of leaves whose probabilities are leaf sums, and this hierarchy is preserved as intervals collapse to points and thirteen relations reduce to five and then three. Each relation further decomposes into correlation-aware temporal primitives in the spirit of CIDOC CRM. The algebra is scale-invariant and separates graded expressions such as "shortly before" from contact relations. All results are Monte-Carlo validated and shipped as an open, tested Python package.

</details>


### [157] [Ownership in AI-Assisted Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20658)
*Megan Wei,Melanie Subbiah,Audrey Lee,Annya Dahmani,Dave Edwards,Helen Edwards,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过定性调查探讨人在与AI协作时的所有权感，发现所有权感取决于协作过程：主导、迭代、重写能保留所有权，单纯审批AI建议则丧失所有权；愿景所有权可延伸至无法独自完成的任务；个人声音丧失和输出理解缺失会削弱所有权；披露AI使用意愿受社区规范和信用抹除恐惧影响，而非实际自豪感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI融入日常任务，需要考察当机器参与生产时，人对作品的所有权感和贡献感会发生什么变化。

Method: 探索性定性调查，参与者描述两个近期与AI完成的任务：一个感觉像自己的，一个不像。

Result: 所有权感取决于协作过程；主导、迭代、重写保留所有权，单纯审批丧失所有权；愿景所有权可延伸至无法独自完成的任务；个人声音丧失和输出理解缺失削弱所有权；披露AI使用意愿与实际自豪感脱节，受社区规范和信用抹除恐惧影响。

Conclusion: 提出若干研究方向，以促进支持人们对自己生活作者身份感的AI发展。

Abstract: When does work done with AI still feel like ours? As AI becomes woven into everyday tasks, we must examine what happens to our sense of ownership and contribution when a machine shares in producing what we make. We report an exploratory qualitative survey in which participants were asked to describe two recent, self-selected tasks completed with AI: one that felt like their own and one that did not. We find that felt ownership depends on the process of collaboration: people disown work when they merely approve AI's suggestions, but retain ownership when they lead, iterate, or rewrite. Ownership can also extend to settings where people own the vision for a project but not the execution; respondents reported high ownership on tasks they could not have completed without AI. Loss of personal voice and a lack of comprehension of the output both erode ownership. Finally, willingness to disclose AI use is often decoupled from actual pride or ownership, and instead shaped by community norms and fear of credit erasure. We propose several research directions as a result of these findings to promote AI development that supports people's sense of authorship over their own lives.

</details>


### [158] [Deep Noir: Autonomous Steering Discovery via Architectural Chronometry in Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20722)
*Frank E. Bobe,Gregory D. Vetaw,Darshan W. Bryner,Matthew G. Cook,Jose L. Salas-Vernis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep Noir框架，利用Logit Lens收敛性和因果头级归因，自动发现LLM激活向量的最佳干预参数，实现跨任务、跨架构的自动化激活向量干预，并在垃圾邮件检测和情感分类任务上显著优于基线，同时揭示了向量干预会线性增加提示词注入攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活向量技术虽然能在推理时修改LLM行为，但确定在哪里干预以及干预强度仍依赖人工搜索，缺乏系统化、自动化的参数发现方法，限制了其在不同任务和模型架构上的泛化能力。

Method: Deep Noir框架包含两个核心组件：1) Logit Lens收敛性分析，用于识别模型内部表示稳定的层；2) 因果头级归因，精确定位对目标行为有因果贡献的注意力头。结合这两者自动发现最佳干预层、头和强度，无需人工调参。

Result: 在三个规模（1B×3、2-3B×2、7-9B×4）共9个模型上验证：垃圾邮件检测任务1B模型提升16.7个百分点（标准差4.7，39次运行），7-9B模型提升21-42个百分点；SST-2情感分类零代码修改提升13.1个百分点。RepE无头掩码版本无改进，Deep Noir全模型显著改进（p<0.01）。另发现干预强度与提示词注入脆弱性呈单调正相关。

Conclusion: Deep Noir通过机制性解释实现了激活向量干预参数的自动化发现，泛化性强跨任务架构有效。但激活向量干预会引入可预测的提示词注入攻击面，脆弱性随干预强度单调增加，这对部署向量干预分类器的智能体系统构成安全隐患，需权衡性能与安全性。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies LLM behavior at inference time, but identifying where and how strongly to steer remains manual. We introduce Deep Noir, a framework that uses Logit Lens convergence and causal head-level attribution to autonomously discover optimal steering parameters. Across three scales (1B x 3, 2-3B x 2, and 7-9B x 4), our engine achieves 16.7 percentage-point improvement on spam at 1B (standard deviation 4.7; 39 runs), with gains increasing to 21 to 42 percentage points at 7-9B across four architectures. On SST-2 sentiment, it achieves a 13.1 percentage-point improvement with zero code changes. Mechanistic grounding enables automated discovery of intervention points that generalize across tasks and architectures. On sentiment, RepE without head masking fails to improve over baseline, while Deep Noir improves all models (p less than 0.01). We further show that steering creates a predictable prompt-injection attack surface whose vulnerability increases monotonically with steering magnitude. This finding is relevant to agent systems deploying steered classifiers.

</details>


### [159] [An Empirical Study of Harness Design for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20804)
*Run-Ze Fan,Zihao Zhang,Simin Ma,Yebowen Hu,Shouju Wang,Kaiqiang Song,Fei Liu,Hamed Zamani,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过固定执行循环、仅变换规划、动作空间和上下文管理三个组件的轻量级编码 harness，在 SWE-Bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上对四种模型进行了 176 组匹配实验。主要发现：上下文管理在预算紧张时价值显著，主要防溢出；分级规则式省略+LLM 摘要效率最高，可恢复省略内容无增益；规划对弱模型提升准确率，对强模型节省成本；预定义工具助弱 bash 模型，强 bash 模型用纯 bash 更低成本。轨迹分析揭示了机制：上下文管理延长轨迹、规划改变终止点、动作空间改变代码编写粒度。为模型/预算感知的 harness 设计提供依据和模块化评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将编码 harness 作为整体系统评估，导致各个组件（规划、动作空间、上下文管理）的单独有效性不清。需要组件级的对比实验，以指导 harness 的模块化设计和针对不同模型能力及预算约束的优化。

Method: 构建轻量级编码 harness，固定执行循环，仅变换三个核心组件：规划、动作空间、上下文管理。在 SWE-Bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench 2.1 基准上评估四种模型，共 176 组匹配设置，涵盖五种上下文管理策略、四种上下文窗口预算，以及规划和动作空间的针对性消融实验。并进行轨迹级分析解释效果机制。

Result: 四大核心发现：(1) 上下文管理随预算收紧价值递增，主要收益来自防止上下文溢出失败；(2) 规则式省略在 LLM 摘要前分级执行的策略整体效率最强，使省略内容可恢复仅增加未被使用的机制，无准确率增益；(3) 规划作用随模型强弱转变：弱模型作准确率支撑，强模型作成本节省，准确率变化极小；(4) 预定义工具提升弱 bash 能力模型性能，而具备 bash 能力的模型用纯 bash 接口即可有效工作，且在命令行为中心的任务上成本大幅降低。轨迹分析显示：上下文管理延长执行轨迹但不显著改变行为，规划改变轨迹终止位置，动作空间改变代码编写粒度。

Conclusion: 研究为模型感知和预算感知的编码 harness 设计提供实证指导，揭示了各组件的独立贡献与交互机制，并建立了用于评估未来 harness 组件的模块化框架。

Abstract: Coding harnesses shape how autonomous coding agents translate model capabilities into long-horizon software-engineering performance, yet existing work typically evaluates harnesses as monolithic systems, leaving the effectiveness of individual components unclear. To enable component-level comparisons, we study this question with a lightweight coding harness whose execution loop is fixed while three components are varied: planning, action space, and context management. Across four models evaluated on SWE-Bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.1, we evaluate 176 matched settings spanning five context-management strategies, four context-window budgets, and targeted ablations of planning and action space. We find that: (1) Context management becomes increasingly valuable as the context-window budget tightens, with most of its benefit coming from preventing context-overflow failures. (2) Staging rule-based elision before LLM-based summarization provides the strongest overall efficiency among the context-management strategies, whereas making elided content recoverable adds machinery that models rarely use and yields no accuracy gain. (3) Planning shifts from an accuracy scaffold for weaker models to a cost saver for stronger models, with little change in accuracy. (4) Predefined tools improve performance for models with weaker bash proficiency, whereas bash-capable models can operate effectively with a bash-only interface and achieve substantially lower cost, especially on command-line-centric tasks. Trajectory-level analysis explains these effects: context management extends execution trajectories without substantially altering agent behavior, planning changes where trajectories stop, and the action space changes the granularity at which code is written. These findings inform model- and budget-aware harness design and provide a modular framework for evaluating future harness components.

</details>


### [160] [Q&A on Any Spreadsheet Requires Interpreting Its Grid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20732)
*Zofia Smoleń*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于语义单元格注释的电子表格分块框架，提升LLM驱动RAG系统的可解释性与生成质量，但指出分类模型因预定义类别有限，难以完全捕捉二维非结构表格的连续结构细微差别，建议未来研究转向降维技术，将二维表格直接展平为一维文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格在LLM驱动的RAG系统中处理困难，因其二维非结构特性导致检索与生成效果受限；语义单元格注释可增强分块可解释性，为下游生成提供更丰富上下文，而非仅提升检索准确率。

Method: 提出一种将任意电子表格按单元格角色注释分割为可解释分块的新框架，利用语义注释识别单元格功能（如标题、数据、公式等），构建结构化文本块供RAG系统使用。

Result: 该框架在实验中超越现有最先进方法，但在处理复杂连续关系和无限潜在单元格角色时遇到硬性性能上限，分类模型无法完美捕捉结构细微差别，即使具备人工级别的注释质量。

Conclusion: 解决电子表格到LLM的瓶颈需超越离散单元格分类，未来应开发降维技术，直接将二维非结构电子表格展平为一维非结构文本，使文本分块更易于下游RAG系统理解与生成。

Abstract: Semantic cell annotation improves chunking interpretability for spreadsheets in LLM-driven RAG systems, aiding answer generation through enriched context rather than improved retrieval accuracy. We propose a novel framework of splitting any spreadsheet into interpretable chunks using cell role annotation. Our framework beats the state of the art, yet it faces a hard ceiling. Spreadsheets are fundamentally two-dimensional unstructured data with continuous relationships and infinite potential cell roles. Because classification models are restricted to finite, pre-defined classes, they cannot perfectly capture this structural nuance, even with human-level annotation. We show that addressing the spreadsheet-to-LLM bottleneck requires moving beyond discrete cell classification. Instead, the field must develop dimensionality-reduction techniques to directly flatten 2D unstructured spreadsheets into 1D unstructured text. Text chunks would be easier for downstream RAG to interpret and generate from.

</details>


### [161] [RAFT: A Stateful Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20754)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Xiaowen Wang,Anupma Sharan,Zhengyi Chen,Chenyu Diana Zhang,Shanshan Yang,Chittibabu Pacharu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RAFT，一种有状态的检索增强框架，将历史故障单抽象为时间线条目的有向链，支持条目级检索与状态匹配，并在合成基准与真实Jira数据上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业客服故障排查依赖从历史案例中检索可执行指引，但现有RAG系统将支持案例视为静态文档，忽略了其多阶段、有状态的演进特性，导致检索效果随案例进展下降。

Method: RAFT将每个已结案案例抽象为时间线条目的有向链，实施条目级检索，匹配活跃案例的中间状态并返回锚定在该状态的父案例轨迹；可选的案例级图通过可配置相似性表示链接案例。评估直接针对检索层，无需完整Agent部署。

Result: 在合成基准（Microsoft Learn Windows Server文档）与真实Apache Jira重复标签数据集上，RAFT在案例进展的每个阶段均显著提升Case Hit指标，优于原版RAG与GraphRAG基线；Jira结果提供迁移到真实案例历史的方向性证据。

Conclusion: RAFT通过建模案例的状态演进实现了更精准的历史指引检索，验证了有状态检索在故障排查场景的有效性，并开源了基准、实现与评估集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Effective troubleshooting agents in enterprise customer support depend on retrieving actionable guidance from similar historical cases, yet existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat support cases as static documents and overlook their multi-stage, stateful nature. We introduce RAFT (Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents), a stateful RAG framework that abstracts each closed historical case into a directed chain of timeline entries and retrieves at the entry level, surfacing cases whose intermediate states match the active case and returning the parent-case trajectory anchored at the matched state; an optional case-level graph links cases through a configurable similarity representation. We evaluate this retrieval layer directly, which, unlike evaluating a full agent system, requires no production deployment. Because public multi-stage troubleshooting data is extremely rare, we pair a synthetic benchmark built from Microsoft Learn Windows Server documentation with real Apache Jira issues carrying human-created duplicate labels. RAFT improves Case Hit over vanilla RAG and GraphRAG baselines at every stage of case progress, with statistically significant gains over the strongest baseline; the Jira results provide directional evidence that the advantage transfers to real case histories. We release our benchmark, implementation, and the Apache Jira evaluation set.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Algebraic Retrieval: Composable Search for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19482)
*Damian Delmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出代数检索，一种让AI智能体在查询时通过数学查询组合搜索策略的方法，实现了相关性标准、资格约束和排序偏好的统一表达，并在公开数据集上验证了执行一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统难以在查询时灵活组合多种检索策略（如对比评分、候选池重排序、加权排序等），且缺乏统一的可编程接口让智能体根据问题动态调整检索程序。

Method: 基于程序化嵌入调制（PEM）构建代数检索框架，暴露向量和评分运算，提供可组合的查询表面；实现对比评分、候选池重排序、加权排序等可组合查询，并提供可执行的SQL和PyTerrier对应实现。

Result: 在11,429文档的Vaswani数据集上，各程序实现选出相同文档集，评分差异低于1e-6；仅有一对并列文档在不同评分路径下排序不同，验证了执行一致性。

Conclusion: 代数检索实现了检索策略的可组合、可编程表达，保证了多种实现路径下的执行一致性，为AI智能体动态构建和修订检索程序提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Algebraic Retrieval lets AI agents compose search strategies at query time. Relevance criteria, eligibility constraints, and ranking preferences can be expressed together in a mathematical query. The query surface exposes available operations, so an agent can combine them for the question at hand and revise a program after inspecting results. We evaluate execution parity, not agent behavior or retrieval quality. Building on Programmatic Embedding Modulation (PEM), which exposes vector and score arithmetic during retrieval, we demonstrate contrastive scoring, candidate-pool reranking, and weighted ranking as composable queries, alongside executable SQL and PyTerrier counterparts. On the public 11,429-document Vaswani fixture, each program's implementations select the same document set with score differences below 1e-6; one tied pair orders differently across scoring paths.

</details>


### [163] [Beyond Similarity through Zero-Token Geometric Graphs for Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19622)
*Zeliang Li,Xiaofen Xing,Kailing Guo,Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Geometric Gain Graph RAG (G$^3$RAG)，一种无需大模型调用即可离线构建图的多跳检索增强生成框架，利用几何增益分数（cosθ·sinθ）捕捉文档表示的方向一致性与正交性，结合密度感知拓扑惩罚与单步受控扩散，实现高效的多跳证据发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密检索倾向于语义相似文档，难以跨越语义鸿沟；基于图的方法依赖昂贵的大模型实体抽取并易受噪声连接干扰。需要一种无大模型参与的图构建方法，同时保证证据的相关性与新颖性。

Method: 1. 离线构建仅含文档节点的图，边权重为几何增益分数 cosθ·sinθ，θ 为文档嵌入向量夹角，联合建模方向一致性与正交性。2. 引入密度感知拓扑惩罚抑制高度连接的中心节点。3. 从过滤后的查询种子出发，单步受控扩散向互补证据扩展。无需大模型调用或生成 token。

Result: 在 MusiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA 三个数据集上，使用 Nv-embed-v2 与 Qwen3-8B-embed 两种嵌入模型评测。G$^3$RAG 在两种嵌入设置下均取得图类基线中最优的平均 F1 与答案-文档命中率，平均 F1 提升高达 4.26 分，MusiQue 上提升 5.76 分，且完全消除实体图构建的 token 成本。

Conclusion: 几何结构能够支撑高效的多跳证据发现，无需依赖大模型进行图构建，为检索增强生成提供了一种低成本、高性能的图增强范式。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) requires evidence that remains relevant to a query while introducing enough novelty to bridge semantic gaps. Dense retrieval tends to concentrate on semantically similar documents, whereas graph-based alternatives often depend on costly Large Language Model (LLM) entity extraction and may propagate through noisy connections. We introduce Geometric Gain Graph RAG (G$^3$RAG), a document-only framework whose offline graph construction uses no LLM calls or generated tokens. G$^3$RAG assigns each edge a geometric gain score, $\cosθ\cdot \sinθ$, that jointly captures directional consistency and orthogonality between document representations. A density-aware topological penalty suppresses highly connected hubs, while single-step controlled diffusion expands from filtered query seeds toward complementary evidence. We evaluate G$^3$RAG on MusiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and HotpotQA using Nv-embed-v2 and Qwen3-8B-embed. G$^3$RAG obtains the best average F1 and answer-document hit rate among the evaluated graph-based baselines in both embedding settings, with gains of up to 4.26 F1 points in average performance and 5.76 points on MusiQue. It also removes the graph-construction token cost incurred by entity-based graph methods. These results show that geometric structure can support efficient multi-hop evidence discovery without LLM-based graph construction. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/G3RAG-99D9/

</details>


### [164] [Self-Evolving Search Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19656)
*Sangam Lee,Wonjae Lee,Sunghwan Kim,Deogyong Kim,Jaehoon Kim,Daye Nam,SeongKu Kang,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SELF-INDEX 是一个让索引自主进化的框架，通过优化器自动诊断检索缺陷、修订索引键并验证，结合查询模拟器主动探索潜在需求，在多种语料库和检索器上持续提升检索性能，优于现有方法，并提升下游应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息检索在 LLM 代理处理复杂任务中日益重要，检索质量高度依赖索引键对文档知识的暴露程度。然而有效的索引表示随检索环境变化，固定优化策略难以持续有效；目前索引演进主要靠人工诊断失败、调整策略、重新处理，效率低且不具备自动化。

Method: 提出 SELF-INDEX 框架，包含优化器和查询模拟器。优化器自主诊断检索短板，选择性修订相关索引键，并在更新前验证修订效果。查询模拟器主动生成潜在查询需求，使索引超越现有查询集进行演进。整个过程无需人工干预。

Result: 在多样化语料库和检索器上，SELF-INDEX 持续提升检索性能，优于现有索引优化方法。同时证明这些收益延伸至下游应用，提升搜索代理的有效性与效率，帮助代理记忆系统检索有用的历史交互。

Conclusion: SELF-INDEX 实现了索引的自主进化，解决了固定优化策略难以适应多变环境和人工驱动演进的局限，为检索系统的自动化优化提供了有效方案，并在实际应用中展现出显著价值。

Abstract: Information retrieval is increasingly important as LLM agents tackle complex tasks involving diverse information needs. Because retrieval relies on an index that represents each document through index keys, retrieval quality depends heavily on how effectively these keys expose the knowledge contained in each document. However, effective index representations vary across retrieval environments, making it difficult for any fixed optimization strategy to perform consistently. Yet evolving an index to its retrieval environment remains largely human-driven, requiring humans to diagnose retrieval failures, refine the optimization strategy, and reprocess the index accordingly. We propose SELF-INDEX, a framework that enables an index to self-evolve without human intervention. Its Optimizer autonomously diagnoses retrieval shortfalls, selectively revises the responsible index keys, and validates each revision before updating the index. Beyond reacting to observed retrieval demands, SELF-INDEX proactively explores additional demands through a Query Simulator, allowing the index to evolve beyond the queries already available for optimization. Across diverse corpora and retrievers, SELF-INDEX consistently improves retrieval performance while outperforming existing index optimization methods. We further show that these benefits extend to downstream applications, improving the effectiveness and efficiency of search agents and helping agent memory systems retrieve useful past interactions.

</details>


### [165] [Dense Feature Representation over Sequence Modeling: A Solution to the KDD Cup 2026 UniRec Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19787)
*Yi Zhang,Weiliang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了KDD Cup 2026腾讯UniRec挑战赛第10名解决方案，通过15步单变量实验将基线AUC从0.8132提升至0.8278，最终提交达到0.8285。消融实验显示：稠密特征表示栈贡献0.0095 AUC，正交化优化器贡献0.0028 AUC，而序列建模组件（合并单流主干、极性通道、辅助头、逐token FFN）均不超过0.0005 AUC。发现验证集与测试集存在时间窗口重叠导致验证AUC虚高约0.014，分布漂移使反记忆和高基数ID特征在验证集上表现反常。结论：大规模CVR预测中，稠密表示和优化器而非精细序列建模驱动AUC提升，必须依赖榜单评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在大规模工业点击转化率（CVR）预测任务中，哪些机制真正能提升举出集AUC，区分有效改进与虚假提升，避免验证集分布偏移导致的误导性结论。

Method: 从官方PCVRHyFormer基线出发，采用15步单变量链式实验逐步改进模型；进行留一消融实验量化各组件贡献；分析行分组训练/验证划分的时间窗口重叠问题，对比验证集与榜单表现，追踪dump-to-dump分布漂移影响。

Result: 测试AUC从0.813237提升至0.827816（+0.0146），最终提交0.828535。消融：移除稠密特征表示栈-0.0095 AUC，移除正交化优化器-0.0028 AUC，序列建模组件均≤0.0005 AUC（在±0.0004随机种子波动范围内）。验证AUC比榜单虚高~0.014，反记忆和高基数ID改动在验证集上符号反转，时间有序重划分后漂移依然存在。

Conclusion: 在3482万记录规模的CVR任务中，稠密特征表示和优化器改进是AUC提升的核心驱动力，而非更精细的序列建模。验证集因时间窗口重叠和分布漂移不可靠，模型评估必须依赖举出集榜单结果。

Abstract: We describe our 10th-place solution to the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge, industrial click-to-conversion (CVR) prediction over 34.82M records, and we ask which mechanisms actually move held-out AUC. Starting from the official PCVRHyFormer baseline, a 15-step single-variable chain raises test AUC from 0.813237 to 0.827816, and our final submission reaches 0.828535. A leave-one-out ablation from the full model attributes the gain: removing the dense-feature representation stack costs 0.0095 AUC and removing the orthogonalized optimizer costs 0.0028, while no sequence-modeling component (merged single-stream backbone, polarity channel, auxiliary head, per-token FFN) costs more than 0.0005, within or adjacent to a $\pm$0.0004 seed band. We also report a generalization hazard: the row-group train/validation split shares one time window, so validation AUC overstates the leaderboard by about 0.014; anti-memorization and high-cardinality-ID changes even invert sign against it, a divergence that traces to dump-to-dump distribution shift and survives a time-ordered re-split. Dense representation and optimization, not finer sequence modeling, drive CVR AUC at this scale, and verdicts must come from the held-out leaderboard.

</details>


### [166] [Reproducing Transparent and Scrutable Recommendations: Exploring Open-Weight Models via Natural-Language User Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19831)
*Noah Mamié,Laurin van den Bergh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是一项复现性研究，验证了基于自然语言用户画像的推荐系统（UPR）的核心发现，并通过消融实验、多种子稳定性测试和机制可解释性分析（nnsight）深入探究。结果显示UPR在重排序协议下性能具有竞争力且提高透明度，但扰动用户画像仅均匀偏移各流派预测评分，无流派选择性效应，排名不变，归因于评分回归目标而非画像接口，排序目标模型表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原论文提出利用生成的自然语言用户画像增强推荐系统的透明度和可审查性，允许用户直接干预画像以定制推荐。本研究旨在复现核心发现，并进一步系统性评估其稳定性、上下文依赖性及内部机制，以验证画像接口是否真正实现细粒度的用户控制。

Method: 复现原论文实验（Amazon Movies & TV、TripAdvisor数据集），进行系统性上下文消融实验、五种随机种子的多种子稳定性测试，并使用nnsight框架进行机制可解释性分析，通过反事实画像扰动和激活转向探究模型内部表示。

Result: 成功复现核心发现：UPR在测试集重排序协议下性能具有竞争力，推荐更透明。但扰动自然语言画像虽改变预测，仅均匀偏移各流派预测评分，无流派选择性效应，排名不变（即使激活转向）。机制分析将此归因于评分回归目标而非画像接口，排序目标模型在该任务上明显优于回归目标模型。

Conclusion: 自然语言用户画像虽提升透明度，但当前基于评分回归的UPR无法实现细粒度的流派级控制，用户干预画像仅导致整体评分平移而非排名变化。未来工作应探索排序目标或其它目标函数，以实现真正可控的推荐。

Abstract: In this reproducibility study, we investigate the transparency and scrutability of recommender systems enhanced by incorporating generated natural-language user profiles that represent user preferences. The original paper explores the synthesis of user profiles from raw user-generated review text across domains such as movies and accommodations (Amazon Movies & TV, TripAdvisor). Crucially, these natural-language user profiles enable direct user interaction and intervention, allowing users to customize recommendations by correcting misattributed preferences or addressing cold-start settings. We successfully reproduce the core findings of the original study. Additionally, we extend the evaluation by conducting systematic context ablation experiments, multi-seed stability across five distinct random seeds to establish statistical reliability, and a mechanistic interpretability analysis using the nnsight framework to probe internal model representations under counterfactual profile perturbations. Our findings verify the original paper's claim that User Profile Recommendation (UPR) achieves competitive performance under its test-set reranking protocol and makes recommendations more transparent. Perturbing the natural-language profiles does change predictions, but it shifts predicted ratings uniformly across genres with no detectable genre-selective effect, leaving rankings unchanged even under direct activation steering. We trace this back to the rating-regression objective rather than the profile interface, with ranking-objective models clearly exceeding in this task.

</details>


### [167] [The Missing Complement: State-Conditioned Minimal Sufficient Evidence for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20050)
*Zhexi Feng,Ruiyi Zhang,Yongbo Yang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向编码代理的'状态条件最小充分证据恢复'任务，构建 SERBench 基准并提出 MSS-Complement 方法，将检索建模为集合构建而非排序，实验显示其在更少 token 下以更高完备率恢复决策所需证据，显著提升下游修复定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索器按段落打相关性分，但代理决策需要的充分性是集合层面属性：排序器易用同一事实的变体填满预算，导致关键事实缺失、决策无支撑。需根据代理当前已观测状态，精准补齐下一决策仍缺的证据集合。

Method: 1. 任务形式化：给定代理状态，恢复紧凑且联合充分的证据集合。2. SERBench：从 45 个仓库采样 500 个保留状态，记录已见上下文，仅当集合覆盖当前决策标注的全部所需事实才计为成功。3. MSS-Complement：三步语义调用——提出联合充分集、搜索其缺失项、返回 4-8 个完整源单元（≤6,144 tokens），将获取建模为集合构建而非排序。

Result: 固定校准配置下，5 项证据完备率 73.0%、8 项 80.6%，显著优于 Qwen3+重排序（61.4%/72.4%）及纯相似度排序基线（66.6%），增益来自集合级策略。无金标准池的冻结仓库源上领先 5.0 点。AMA-Bench 上答案提示词缩小 76.2%，准确率仍比该基准自带记忆代理高 2.08 点。去除一个必需组分别使两种执行器的修复定位精度下降 12.3 与 11.1 点。

Conclusion: 面向代理的检索应重新定义为'恢复决策尚缺的证据'，而非'重排序与问题相似的段落'；集合级补全策略比逐段相关性排序更能保障决策所需的充分性。

Abstract: A coding agent halfway through an issue has already read much of what a retriever ranks highest. Relevance is scored per passage, but sufficiency belongs to the set: a ranker can fill its budget with variants of one required fact and leave the decision unsupported. We formulate state-conditioned minimal sufficient evidence recovery: given a captured agent state, recover a compact evidence combination that supplies the support its next decision still lacks. SERBench measures this on 500 held-out states from 45 repositories, recording what the agent has seen and crediting only sets that cover every fact the current decision was annotated to require. MSS-Complement treats acquisition as set construction, not ranking. Three semantic calls propose a jointly sufficient set, search for what it lacks, and return 4-8 intact source units within 6,144 tokens. One configuration, fixed on calibration data, recovers a complete set for 73.0% of those states at five items and 80.6% at eight, against 61.4% and 72.4% for Qwen3 embedding with reranking. A matched control ranking by similarity alone reaches 66.6%, placing the gain in the set-level policy, not the computation. From frozen repository source with no gold-derived pool, the lead is 5.0 points. On AMA-Bench it answers from a 76.2% smaller answer prompt, with accuracy 2.08 points above that benchmark's own memory agent. Removing one required group from an otherwise complete set costs 12.3 and 11.1 points of repair-localization precision under two executors. Retrieval for agents is better posed as recovering what a decision lacks than re-ranking what an issue resembles.

</details>


### [168] [Think Thrice Before Reranking: Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20131)
*Lijun Liu,Zhengzong Chen,Wenyan Li,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT-Rank框架，通过多视角推理轨迹建模和渐进式策略优化，解决单一推理路径导致的重排序不稳定问题，在推理密集型和传统检索基准上均取得优异性能，4B模型甚至超越多数7B和32B重排器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推理重排序方法主要依赖单一推理轨迹，导致排名易受推理错误影响，且难以建模文档相关性的多面性信号，亟需更鲁棒的多视角推理整合机制。

Method: 提出MERIT-Rank框架：1) 构建多轨迹推理空间(MTRS)，从多视角评估查询-文档相关性；2) 设计联合重排器整合多条推理路径为统一排名决策；3) 开发渐进式排序策略优化(PRPO)，通过分阶段优化目标稳定推理轨迹并持续提升排序质量。

Result: 在推理密集型(BRIGHT)和传统检索基准上，MERIT-Rank持续超越竞争基线；值得注意的是，4B参数模型性能优于大多数7B甚至32B的重排序模型。

Conclusion: 多视角推理整合与渐进式训练策略能显著提升LLM重排序的鲁棒性和效率，小模型通过更优的推理机制可超越大模型，为高效文本重排序提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reasoning-based reranking with Large Language Models (LLMs) has shown promising improvements in text ranking. However, current methods predominantly rely on a single reasoning trajectory, resulting in rankings that are susceptible to reasoning errors and inherently constrained in modeling the multifaceted signals underlying document relevance. To resolve this dilemma, we propose MERIT-Rank(Multi-perspective Evidence and Reasoning Integration for Text Reranking), a framework that models complementary reasoning trajectories to improve reranking robustness. MERIT-Rank formulates a Multi-Trajectory Reasoning Space (MTRS) that evaluates query-document relevance from multiple perspectives and introduces a joint reranker that consolidates these reasoning paths into a unified ranking decision. We further develop Progressive Rank Policy Optimization (PRPO), a progressive training framework that stabilizes reasoning trajectories while continually improving ranking quality through staged optimization objectives. Experiments on both reasoning-intensive and traditional retrieval benchmarks show that MERIT-Rank consistently achieves superior performance over competitive baselines. The 4B model notably outperforms most 7B and even 32B rerankers on BRIGHT.

</details>


### [169] [FacetCRS: Multi-Faceted Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20175)
*Yongsen Zheng,Ziliang Chen,Jinghui Qin,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出FacetCRS框架，通过多面偏好学习在对话推荐系统中刺破过滤气泡，将用户偏好建模为实体、词汇、上下文和评论四个维度，实现了最优的推荐质量和气泡缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注静态推荐场景下的过滤气泡问题，但真实在线推荐中，用户-系统反馈回路会导致过滤气泡随时间持续加剧。对话推荐系统（CRS）通过自然语言交互提供了及时干预的机会，但缺乏能捕捉多样化动态偏好的方法。

Method: 提出FacetCRS端到端框架，将用户偏好自动建模为四个维度：实体面、词汇面、上下文面和评论面。通过自适应学习各层级偏好面的表示及多类型外部知识，在对话过程中捕捉多样化动态偏好，从而刺破过滤气泡。

Result: 在两个公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，FacetCRS在缓解过滤气泡和提升推荐质量方面均达到了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 多面偏好学习能有效应对对话推荐中的过滤气泡问题，通过细粒度偏好建模和自适应表示学习，实现了推荐多样性与准确性的平衡，为动态推荐环境下的气泡缓解提供了新范式。

Abstract: The filter bubble is a notorious issue in Recommender Systems (RSs), which describes the phenomenon whereby users are exposed to a limited and narrow range of information or content that reinforces their existing dominant preferences and beliefs. This results in a lack of exposure to diverse and varied content. Many existing works have predominantly examined filter bubbles in static or relatively-static recommendation settings. However, filter bubbles will be continuously intensified over time due to the feedback loop between the user and the system in the real-world online recommendation. To address these issues, we propose a novel paradigm, Multi-Facet Preference Learning for Pricking Filter Bubbles in Conversational Recommender System (FacetCRS), which aims to burst filter bubbles in the conversational recommender system (CRS) through timely user-item interactions via natural language conversations. By considering diverse user preferences and intentions, FacetCRS automatically model user preference into multi-facets, including entity-, word-, context-, and review-facet, to capture diverse and dynamic user preferences to prick filter bubbles in the CRS. It is an end-to-end CRS framework to adaptively learn representations of various levels of preference facet and diverse types of external knowledge. Extensive experiments on two publicly available benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance in mitigating filter bubbles and enhancing recommendation quality in CRS.

</details>


### [170] [Reasoning Quality Matters: Combating Reasoning Collapse in LLM-based Embedding Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20563)
*Zihan Gong,Xiaohan Ye,Jiangchao Yao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CoFree 是一个两阶段框架，解决 LLM 嵌入学习中的推理崩溃问题，通过参考引导的微调恢复推理能力，再用双重奖励（嵌入导向与推理导向）的强化学习实现推理引导的检索优化，在 22 个数据集上比 Qwen3-Embedding-4B 平均提升 2.8 nDCG@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 嵌入方法要么被动提取特征，要么利用指令跟随推理，但针对嵌入目标的特化会抑制有用推理或产生与检索无关的文本，称为推理崩溃。需要一种方法在优化嵌入的同时保持高质量推理。

Method: 两阶段：第一阶段参考引导监督微调，恢复推理能力并保持基础嵌入模型的表征强度；第二阶段引入双重奖励（嵌入导向奖励和推理导向奖励）进行强化学习，实现终点耦合优化，将嵌入学习从静态对齐转变为高质量推理引导的检索搜索过程。

Result: CoFree-4B 在 MTEB 和 BRIGHT 的 22 个数据集上平均绝对提升 2.8 nDCG@10 超过 Qwen3-Embedding-4B；真实检索系统在线实验也显示持续增益。

Conclusion: CoFree 通过渐进式整合推理与嵌入优化，有效缓解推理崩溃，实现了推理质量与检索性能的双重提升，为 LLM 嵌入学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown strong potential for producing context-rich text embeddings for retrieval. Most existing methods either treat embedding learning as passive feature extraction or exploit LLM reasoning through instruction following for better embedding optimization. However, specialization toward embedding objectives can suppress useful reasoning generation or produce retrieval-irrelevant text. We refer to these two forms of degradation as reasoning collapse. To address this issue, we propose CoFree (Collapse-Free Reasoning Embedding), a two-stage framework that progressively integrates LLM reasoning into query and document embedding optimization while preserving reasoning quality. At the first stage, CoFree applies reference-guided supervised fine-tuning to restore the reasoning ability and retain representational strength of the foundation embedding model. At the second stage, we introduce dual rewards, an embedding-oriented reward and a reasoning-oriented reward, to guarantee fine-grained reasoning of the relevance toward the embedding goal in reinforcement learning. This endpoint-coupled optimization transforms embedding learning from static alignment into a high-quality reasoning-guided search process for retrieval. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of CoFree, with CoFree-4B achieving an average absolute improvement of 2.8 nDCG@10 points over Qwen3-Embedding-4B across 22 datasets from MTEB and BRIGHT. Online experiments in a real-world retrieval system further show consistent gains. Code, RTED, and model checkpoints will be made publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Generative Query Suggestion via Intent Coverage and Query-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19209)
*Xinpeng Liu,Lu Ma,Jiayi Qiao,Mengyu Zhou,Linglong Li,Xiaofeng Bian,Haonan Chen,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于意图驱动的查询建议框架，通过双阶段优化（意图感知多样性建模和查询级信用分配）提升生成查询的质量、多样性及意图覆盖，线上线下实验验证了点击率、查询质量和意图覆盖的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式查询建议旨在预测用户意图并推荐相关后续查询，核心挑战在于生成的查询集既要保证单个查询的实用性，又要覆盖不同意图以提升用户参与度。

Method: 提出双阶段优化框架：第一阶段构建意图对齐的监督微调数据，并引入意图感知多样性奖励优化意图覆盖；第二阶段采用查询级信用分配机制，将单个查询的质量信号路由至对应token，同时在查询集层面共享多样性信号。

Result: 在大规模生产数据集上进行线上A/B测试和离线评估，结果显示点击率、查询质量和意图覆盖均显著提升。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了生成式查询建议中实用性与多样性的平衡问题，为工业界大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative query suggestion aims to enhance user engagement by anticipating user intents and recommending relevant follow-up queries. A central challenge is to generate slates whose individual queries are useful while the slate covers distinct intents. We propose an Intent-Driven Query Suggestion Framework with dual-stage optimization. First, intent-aware diversity modeling constructs intent-aligned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data and uses an Intent-Aware Diversity Reward to optimize intent coverage. Second, query-level credit assignment routes individual quality signals to the corresponding query tokens while sharing a slate-level diversity signal across the slate. Experiments on a large-scale production dataset, including online A/B testing and offline evaluation, show improvements in click-through rate, query quality, and intent coverage.

</details>


### [172] [Layer-wise Curriculum Learning for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19213)
*Donggeon Lee,Dooyeon Na,Seungmin Oh,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层课程学习方法用于高效大语言模型压缩，通过将模型划分为多个层段，实现从易到难的知识蒸馏，结合特征缓存与多线程策略解决层间特征错位，显著降低显存占用与训练时间，在BERT、GPT-2、LLaMA及Qwen等模型上达最先进压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM压缩方法面临知识蒸馏难度大、累积误差导致收敛不稳定、层间特征错位及GPU利用率低等问题，亟需一种计算高效、收敛稳定且显存友好的压缩范式。

Method: 1. 分层课程学习：将模型划分为多层段，从浅层到深层逐段蒸馏，由易到难优化；2. 理论分析累积误差现象，证明分层策略能加速收敛并稳定蒸馏过程；3. 特征缓存+多线程策略：缓存教师模型中间特征，多线程预取解决层间特征错位，最大化GPU利用率。

Result: 在BERT和GPT-2上，显存占用与训练时间均降低50%以上；在LLaMA家族及Qwen模型上，同等训练时长下超越其他剪枝方法，且显存占用更低，达最先进压缩性能。

Conclusion: 分层课程学习结合特征缓存与多线程策略，有效解决了LLM压缩中的知识迁移难、收敛不稳定、特征错位及资源利用率低等核心问题，实现了高性能、高效率、低资源消耗的模型压缩，为大模型部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce layer-wise curriculum learning for efficient LLM compression. The proposed method facilitates the knowledge transfer from the teacher model to the student model, utilizing a curriculum learning approach that begins with easier optimization tasks and progressively tackles harder ones. In order to adopt the layer-wise learning in LLM compression, we partition the whole model into multiple segments consisting of layers, thereby enabling more computationally efficient knowledge transfer for LLMs. Based on our theoretical analysis of cumulative error phenomenon, layer-wise curriculum learning accelerates convergence while stabilizing the knowledge transfer process. In addition, we present a feature caching method with a multi-threading strategy to efficiently address feature misalignment across layers, maximizing GPU utilization. Consequently, our method exhibits advanced model compression performance, as well as high computational efficiency in terms of minimized memory usage and short training hours. Experiments on multiple datasets show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing GPU memory usage and training hours by more than 50\% on BERT and GPT-2. Moreover, it outperforms the other pruning methods on LLaMA-family and Qwen models under the same training hours, with a lower GPU memory footprint.

</details>


### [173] [Block Parallelism For Efficient Distributed Long-Context Diffusion Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19242)
*Tarun Suresh,Pranshu Chaturvedi,Hangoo Kang,Parth Shroff,Ishan S. Khare,Hermann Kumbong,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对块扩散语言模型（BDLM）在长上下文训练中面临的通信和显存瓶颈，提出了块并行（BP）和上下文分片块并行（CSBP）两种新的分布式并行维度。CSBP 将共享的干净序列跨秩分片，保持损坏 K/V 及梯度本地化，避免了干净前缀的复制，同时保留 BDLM 训练语义。实验表明，在 16 张 H200 GPU、256K 上下文下，CSBP 相对最佳基线吞吐提升 1.18-1.45 倍（SFT）和 1.27-1.33 倍（自回归转 BDLM），峰值 HBM 不增反降；512K 时全模型加速 1.61 倍。在 8 张 H100 上，DFlash2 推测解码器训练分别加速 2.48 倍（512K）和 7.59 倍（1M）。同等 12 小时 DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT 运行中，CSBP 在 SWE-bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench Lite 上每个检查点的通过率均更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDLM 结合了块间自回归依赖与块内并行去噪，但长上下文训练受限于分布式注意力通信开销和激活显存。传统上下文并行（CP）按位置分片合并后的干净+损坏序列，需通信共享的干净 K/V 以及各块特有的损坏 K/V 及其梯度，导致通信量大、显存占用高，难以扩展到超长上下文。

Method: 1. 块并行（BP）：利用 BDLM 目标在目标块上可分离的性质，将每个损坏块的计算分配到单独的秩，实现块级并行。2. 上下文分片块并行（CSBP）：在 BP 基础上，进一步将共享的干净序列跨这些秩分片。CSBP 使损坏 K/V 及梯度保持本地，避免干净前缀在多秩间复制，且完全保留 BDLM 原始训练语义。

Result: 在 16 张 H200 GPU、256K 上下文下，CSBP 对比最佳基线：SFT 吞吐提升 1.18-1.45 倍，自回归模型转 BDLM 提升 1.27-1.33 倍，峰值 HBM 持平或下降；512K 时全模型加速 1.61 倍。在 8 张 H100 上训练 DFlash2 推测解码器：512K 加速 2.48 倍，1M 加速 7.59 倍。同等 12 小时 DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT 实验中，CSBP 在 SWE-bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench Lite 上每个训练检查点的通过率均优于基线。

Conclusion: CSBP 是一种高效的分布式并行策略，能显著降低 BDLM 长上下文训练的通信开销和显存占用，同时保持训练语义不变，实现了吞吐与模型质量的双重提升，为超长上下文扩散语言模型的训练提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models (BDLMs) combine autoregressive dependencies across blocks with parallel denoising within blocks, but long-context training is constrained by distributed attention communication and activation memory. Conventional context parallelism (CP) shards the combined clean-plus-corrupted sequence by position, communicating shared clean K/V together with block-specific corrupted K/V and their gradients. We observe that the BDLM objective separates over target blocks. We introduce block parallelism (BP), a new distributed parallelism dimension that assigns each corrupted-block computation to one rank. To scale BP to long contexts, we introduce context-sharded block parallelism (CSBP), which also shards the shared clean sequence across those ranks. CSBP keeps corrupted K/V and gradients local, avoids replicated clean prefixes, and preserves BDLM training semantics. On 16 H200 GPUs at 256K context, CSBP improves throughput over the best baseline by 1.18-1.45x for supervised fine-tuning and 1.27-1.33x for conversion of autoregressive models to BDLMs, while matching or reducing peak HBM. Full-model speedup reaches 1.61x at 512K. On eight H100 GPUs, CSBP accelerates DFlash2 speculative-decoder training by 2.48x at 512K and 7.59x at 1M. In matched 12-hour DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT runs, CSBP achieves higher pass rates at every trained checkpoint on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench Lite. Code: https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/Turbo-dLLM

</details>


### [174] [Randomized SVD Approximations for Spectral Co-Clustering of Word-Document Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19243)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对谱协聚类在高维词-文档矩阵上依赖 SVD 导致的计算昂贵问题，提出了两种随机近似方法：基于随机投影的随机 SVD 以及结合部分 SVD 与逐元素随机采样的混合方法。实验表明随机投影法在各类稀疏度设置下表现更稳健可靠，采样法在稠密矩阵上优势明显但在稀疏文本数据上收益有限，结论指出应根据数据底层结构选择近似策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱协聚类虽能有效挖掘词-文档矩阵的潜在结构，但标准方法依赖全 SVD 分解，在高维大规模文本数据上计算开销过大，亟需更高效的近似算法。

Method: 1) 随机投影法：利用随机投影矩阵构造低维近似，再对投影后的矩阵执行 SVD，实现随机 SVD 加速。2) 采样混合法：先计算部分奇异向量，再对矩阵元素进行逐元素随机采样，结合部分 SVD 结果得到近似。两方法均适用于文档簇与词簇数目不等的二分文本数据。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上，两种方法均显著降低运行时间。随机投影法在不同稀疏度下均表现稳健，是更可靠的近似；采样法在稠密矩阵上加速效果最好，但在已稀疏的文本数据上改进有限。

Conclusion: 随机近似谱协聚类的算法选择应依据数据的内在结构（特别是稀疏度）：随机投影法具有更广泛的适用性，采样法更适合稠密矩阵场景。

Abstract: Spectral co-clustering is a useful tool for discovering latent structure in word-document matrices, but its reliance on singular value decomposition (SVD) can make standard formulations expensive on high-dimensional data. This paper presents two randomized approximations for normalized spectral co-clustering of bipartite text data when the numbers of document and word clusters may differ. The first method uses randomized SVD through random projection, while the second combines partial SVD with element-wise random sampling. Across real-world and synthetic datasets, both methods reduce runtime relative to the full-SVD baseline, but their behavior depends on matrix sparsity. The random projection method is the more reliable approximation across the tested settings, whereas the sampling-based method is most useful on denser matrices and provides limited benefit on already sparse text data. These results show that randomized approximations for spectral co-clustering should be selected according to the underlying structure of the data.

</details>


### [175] [Radio-Frequency Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19279)
*Zhihui Gao,Shi-Yuan Ma,Yiran Chen,Dirk Englund,Tingjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了射频卷积神经网络（RF-CNN），利用无线通信设备中现有的频率混频器在频域执行卷积运算，无需额外硬件即可在边缘设备上高效运行深度CNN，能耗低至0.72 fJ/MAC，性能接近全精度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备（智能手机、穿戴设备、无人机等）受限于尺寸、重量、功耗和成本（SWaP-C），难以部署现代神经网络所需的计算能力。现有方案需额外添加边缘加速器硬件，进一步加剧资源约束。无线射频前端中的混频器天然在时域执行乘法，等价于频域卷积，可复用现有通信硬件实现AI推理。

Method: 将多通道卷积映射到频率音调上，利用无源混频器单次通过即可执行卷积运算。权重通过空中接口空中传输，模拟硬件与通信功能共享。系统仅在数据准备和读出阶段消耗能量，避免了数字处理器的高功耗乘累加运算。

Result: 实验演示RF-CNN可运行高达2640万参数、9层的深度CNN，覆盖无线信号分类、图像分类和可控图像生成任务，性能接近全精度基线。能耗仅为0.72 fJ/MAC，比额外数字处理器低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 已部署的无线基础设施可为数十亿连接设备带来高效、最先进的AI推理能力，无需额外硬件成本，实现了计算与通信的深度融合。

Abstract: Running artificial intelligence (AI) models directly on edge devices such as smartphones, wearables, and drones offers low latency, pervasive scalability, and data privacy, but these devices rarely carry the computing capability that modern neural networks demand. Edge accelerators have been developed in response, yet each adds computing hardware to devices already constrained in size, weight, power, and cost (SWaP-C). An alternative lies in what these devices already carry: the frequency mixer in every wireless radio multiplies signals in time, natively performing convolution in the frequency domain. Here we introduce radio-frequency convolutional neural networks (RF-CNNs), which repurpose existing communication hardware for CNN inference. Multi-channel convolutions are mapped onto frequency tones for a passive mixer to execute in a single pass. We experimentally demonstrate that RF-CNN runs deep CNNs up to 26.4 million parameters and nine layers from classification of wireless signals and images to controllable image generation, close to full-precision performance. Because the weights arrive over the air and the analog hardware is shared with communication, the edge device spends energy only on data preparation and readout-down to 0.72 femtojoules per multiply-accumulate, two orders of magnitude less than it would cost on an added digital processor. These results suggest that deployed wireless infrastructure can bring efficient, state-of-the-art AI inference to the billions of devices it already connects.

</details>


### [176] [Learning-Induced Dynamical Transition in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19288)
*Varun Vaidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种非平衡动力学平均场理论(DMFT)，描述循环神经网络(RNN)学习过程中从混沌动力学向稳定任务依赖动力学的转变，揭示反馈驱动学习通过有效反馈强度演化引发分岔，并定量预测训练期间网络输出的时间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解RNN学习如何从根本上重塑其潜在动力学，将初始混沌活动转化为稳定的任务依赖行为，并建立描述这一非平衡学习过程的理论框架。

Method: 开发非平衡动力学平均场理论(DMFT)，分析慢反馈驱动学习产生的演化有效反馈强度，推导学习全程的双时间相关函数，识别临界反馈强度和依赖学习率的临界时间。

Result: 理论预测网络从混沌到稳定动力学的转变源于有效动力学景观被逐渐增长的学习反馈结构逐步变形，确定分岔点，且理论预测的训练期间网络输出时间演化与数值模拟定量一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为RNN学习动力学提供理论基础，表明学习过程可视为有效反馈强度驱动的分岔过程，DMFT能准确捕捉学习全程的动力学演化。

Abstract: Learning in recurrent neural networks can fundamentally reshape their underlying dynamics, transforming initially chaotic activity into stable task-dependent behavior. We develop a non-equilibrium dynamical mean-field theory(DMFT) to describe this transition during learning. We show that a slow feedback-driven learning process generates an evolving effective feedback strength that drives the network through a transition from chaotic to stable dynamics defined by a bifurcation of the DMFT solution. By deriving the two-time correlation function throughout learning, we identify a critical feedback strength and a corresponding learning rate dependent critical time separating these regimes. The transition arises from the progressive deformation of an effective dynamical landscape by the growing learned feedback structure. Starting from the untrained state, the theory predicts the time evolution of the network output during training and shows quantitative agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [177] [Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes for Privacy-Preserving Modeling of Heterogeneous Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19337)
*Xianjian Xie,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了个性化联邦分层高斯过程（pFedHGP），用于异构客户端间的概率回归与分类。每个客户端的潜在函数分解为共享全局组件、共享全局核结构的客户端特定偏差、灵活局部残差三部分。利用稀疏诱导变量近似和联邦变分推理保持原始数据本地化，服务器仅同步低维共享组件统计量，提供完整预测分布以支持不确定性感知决策。在冲压吨位监测故障分类和联邦空气质量建模中取得优异表现，并揭示了与多输出高斯过程的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决数据分布在异构客户端间的概率回归与分类问题，既要保持数据本地化（隐私保护），又要实现个性化建模并提供不确定性量化，以支持风险敏感决策。

Method: 个性化联邦分层高斯过程（pFedHGP）：将每个客户端的潜在函数分解为（i）共享全局组件，（ii）共享全局核结构的客户端特定偏差，（iii）灵活局部残差。采用稀疏诱导变量近似和联邦变分推理，原始数据保留在本地，服务器仅聚合共享全局组件的低维统计量。模型输出完整预测分布，支持不确定性感知决策。此外，从瞬时线性混合模型视角将层级结构与多输出高斯过程联系起来，适用于相关传感器建模。

Result: 在冲压吨位监测故障分类任务中，仅使用 13.77% 的标注循环即实现完美分类；在联邦空气质量建模中，无需中心化站点级时间序列即可恢复地理区域。实验验证了 pFedHGP 在异构联邦环境下的有效性。

Conclusion: pFedHGP 通过分层高斯过程分解实现了个性化联邦学习，兼顾全局知识共享与客户端特定适应，提供校准良好的不确定性估计，在工业故障检测和环境监测等实际应用中表现优异，且理论上与多输出高斯过程建立联系，扩展了联邦概率建模的适用范围。

Abstract: We present Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes (pFedHGP) for probabilistic regression and classification when data are distributed across heterogeneous clients. Each client's latent function decomposes into (i) a shared global component, (ii) a client-specific deviation that shares the global kernel structure, and (iii) a flexible local residual. Sparse inducing-variable approximations and federated variational inference keep raw data local while the server synchronizes only low-dimensional statistics for the shared component. Full predictive distributions support uncertainty-aware decisions. In application studies, pFedHGP attains perfect fault classification in press tonnage monitoring using 13.77% of labeled cycles and recovers geographic zones in federated air-quality modeling without centralizing station-level time series. An Instantaneous Linear Mixing Model viewpoint links the hierarchy to multi-output Gaussian processes for correlated sensors.

</details>


### [178] [How to Guide Your Language Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19356)
*Rohit Dilip,Tianrong Chen,Yuyang Wang,David Van Valen,Joshua Susskind,Miguel Angel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出探针引导方法，利用冻结扩散模型的内部状态构建引导信号，无需推理时额外前向传播，在连续扩散语言模型上实现无条件生成SOTA性能，并揭示了autoguidance的实际机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有autoguidance方法需要推理时额外前向传播且机制理解不足，需要一种更高效、可靠的引导方法来改进扩散语言模型，同时确保弱强模型共享相似动力学。

Method: 探针引导：使用冻结的预训练扩散模型内部状态（探针）构建引导信号，原理类似autoguidance但消除推理时额外前向传播，通过探针确保弱模型与强模型共享相似动力学轨迹。

Result: 1) 无条件生成达到新SOTA；2) 1.7B扩散语言模型在多项选择问答基准上持续提升；3) 发现弱模型必须来自训练低熵区域才能使autoguidance有效。

Conclusion: 探针引导为改进扩散语言模型提供实用高效方案，同时揭示autoguidance的核心机制在于弱模型需处于训练低熵区域，为该领域提供理论洞见。

Abstract: We introduce a new method to guide flow matching models. Our approach, which we call probe guidance, uses the frozen internal states of an existing diffusion model to construct a guidance signal. This works using a similar principle as autoguidance, but eliminates the need for an additional forward pass at inference time and provides a reliable path to ensure that the weak and strong model share similar dynamics. We apply and benchmark this method on continuous diffusion language models, where probe guidance sets a new state-of-the-art performance on unconditional generation. When applied to a 1.7B diffusion language model, probe guidance consistently improves on multiple choice question answering benchmarks. Using our probes, we study the traditional autoguidance setting where the strong model is a weak checkpoint, and find that the weak model must come from a low-entropy region of training. These findings both provide a practical way to improve diffusion language models and shed light on the actual mechanism behind autoguidance, which is currently poorly understood.

</details>


### [179] [Smart Insole Human Activity Recognition for Continuous Monitoring in Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19359)
*Edwin Rios,Antony Garcia,Fengpei Yuan,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无线智能鞋垫平台和机器学习工作流，利用足底压力和惯性信号识别坐、站、行走及不稳定行走，基于参与者独立的交叉验证，HGB模型在双足融合下宏观F1达0.98，证明低轮廓鞋垫感知可用于未见参与者的活动状态推断，为老年护理跌倒预防奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒常先于移动、平衡和姿势转换的变化，需要一种无创、低轮廓的可穿戴系统，能从足底压力和惯性测量中推断活动状态，以实现活动监测和跌倒预防。

Method: 开发集成16个有源压力传感点和六维IMU（三轴加速度+角速度）的无线智能鞋垫，从15名健康成人采集80Hz数据，经重叠窗口分割。先用分层10折交叉验证筛选窗口长度和候选模型家族，再用参与者独立的5折分层组交叉验证（确保同一参与者的所有窗口在同一折）评估最终性能。对比了Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting (HGB) 和紧凑型1D-CNN。

Result: 在参与者独立协议下，HGB左脚宏观F1=0.954，右脚=0.959，双足融合=0.980。1D-CNN在相同折上未显著优于HGB（p=0.0625）。表明低轮廓鞋垫感知能对未见训练参与者推断活动状态。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了基于足底压力和IMU的无线智能鞋垫平台及机器学习流程，证明其可泛化至未见参与者，为老年护理中的活动监测和跌倒预防提供了可行基础。

Abstract: Falls in older adults are often preceded by changes in mobility, balance, and postural transitions. This paper presents a wireless smart insole platform and machine-learning workflow for recognizing sitting, standing, walking, and unstable walking from plantar-pressure and inertial signals. Each insole integrates 16 active pressure-sensing locations and a six-dimensional IMU stream consisting of tri-axial acceleration and angular velocity. Data were collected from 15 healthy adults at 80~Hz and segmented into overlapping windows. Window length and candidate model families were first screened with stratified 10-fold cross-validation; the primary performance estimate was then obtained with participant-independent 5-fold Stratified Group cross-validation, ensuring that all windows from a participant remained in a single fold. Under this protocol, Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting (HGB) achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.954 and 0.959 for the left and right feet, respectively, and 0.980 with bilateral sensing. A compact 1D-CNN evaluated with the same participant-independent folds did not significantly outperform HGB ($p=0.0625$). The results show that low-profile footwear sensing can infer activity state from pressure and IMU measurements for participants unseen during training, establishing a basis for activity monitoring and fall prevention in elderly care.

</details>


### [180] [Stiefel Attention: When the Geometry of Transformer Projection Matrices Dominates Optimizer Choice---and When It Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19363)
*Rubén Darío Guerrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将注意力机制中的查询和键投影矩阵约束在Stiefel流形上，并使用一种带有信任区域和极性回缩的Riemannian Adam优化器进行训练。理论分析证明了该更新规则的优良性质，实验表明在模块化算术grokking任务和CIFAR-10补丁任务上显著优于基线，且性能提升主要归因于步长尺度不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制中的查询和键投影矩阵通常使用欧几里得优化器训练，无几何约束，导致权重衰减引起的几何坍塌。作者希望通过将投影矩阵约束在Stiefel流形上，保持正交性，使学习到的注意力几何结构在权重衰减驱动的坍塌周期中存活。

Method: 将WQ, WK约束在Stiefel流形St(d,r)上，设计Riemannian Adam优化器：每帧一个标量二阶矩，步长由信任区域限制，使用极性回缩。证明了四个命题：更新是嵌入度量下最速下降、梯度尺度无关、良好条件、精确O(d)-等变。第五个命题揭示机制：权重衰减在Stiefel流形上Riemannian梯度为零，因为W = W I_r位于法向空间。

Result: 在模块化算术grokking任务上，单次运行在epoch 20,000达到97.0%验证准确率，基线仅61.1%。在CIFAR-10补丁任务上，12组配对实验平均提升8.98个百分点 (t=60.6, 12/12胜)，且差距随数据量增大而扩大而非侵蚀。消融实验显示增益完全来自步长尺度不变性，投影算子和等变性无可测量贡献。负面结果：规范移除不能解释方法，因为损失不变方向无梯度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过流形约束和Riemannian优化有效保护注意力几何结构免受权重衰减破坏，实现显著性能提升，核心在于步长的尺度不变性而非等变性或投影本身。

Abstract: The query and key projections $\WQ,\WK$ in attention are almost always trained by Euclidean optimizers with no constraint on their geometry. We constrain them to the Stiefel manifold and optimize them there with a Riemannian Adam that carries one scalar second moment per frame, caps its step by a trust region, and retracts polarly. Four propositions prove this update is steepest descent in the embedded metric, independent of gradient scale, well conditioned, and exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant, each certified numerically in \texttt{float64}. A fifth supplies the mechanism: weight decay has \emph{identically zero} Riemannian gradient on $\St(d,r)$, since $W = W I_r$ lies in the normal space, so the learned attention geometry survives the collapse cycles that decay drives through the rest of the model. On modular arithmetic grokking, a single run holds $97.0\%$ validation accuracy at epoch 20\,000 against the baseline's $61.1\%$---an unstable endpoint we report as evidence for the mechanism rather than as an effect size. On CIFAR-10 patches the same rule gains $\mathbf{+8.98}$\,pp over 12 paired starts ($t{=}60.6$, $12/12$), and the gap widens with data rather than eroding. The step rule earns this: a fixed-step Riemannian update is degree one in the gradient, so it moves $24$--$40\times$ less per step than an identically shaped AdamW matrix---its frames barely leave their initialization, and freezing them outright costs only $0.28$\,pp. An ablation credits the whole gain to making the step scale free, and nothing measurable to the projector or to equivariance. A negative result sharpens the account: gauge removal cannot motivate the method, because a direction along which the loss is invariant carries no gradient at all.

</details>


### [181] [Machine-Learning Assessment of the Predictive Value of Inflammatory Biomarkers for Cognitive Impairment in an Older Hispanic Adult Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19374)
*Antony Garcia,Gabrielle Britton,Alcibiades Villarreal,Diana Oviedo,Giselle Rangel,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对小样本临床表格数据，提出一种防泄漏的阈值似然朴素贝叶斯分类器，识别出I-309/CCL1作为认知障碍预测的关键可解释特征，AUC提升0.110，经多重校正后仍显著，模型以阈值表和条件概率表形式呈现，待外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本临床表格数据不适用深度学习，集成模型难以解释；统计显著性不等于预测效用，亟需一种可解释、防泄漏的建模方法来挖掘真正具有预测增量价值的生物标志物。

Method: 使用PARI-HD队列(n=165)数据，采用防泄漏的阈值似然伯努利/分类朴素贝叶斯分类器。在每个训练折中，连续预测变量通过监督卡方衍生离散状态，收入作为分类似然纳入。所有数据依赖步骤均在重复分层10折交叉验证(30次重复)内完成。采用配对DeLong检验评估AUC增量，Benjamini-Hochberg校正控制多重比较，并通过200次随机分区评估稳健性。

Result: 基线人口学模型ROC-AUC为0.630±0.017。I-309使AUC增加0.110，100%重复中配对DeLong检验p<0.05。预设主要分析中，固定分区DeLong p=0.0018，200次随机分区中位数p=0.0011。探索性分析中，I-309在冻结分区上BH校正后q=0.032，85%随机分区满足q<0.05；其余17个候选标志物均无可靠增量预测价值。

Conclusion: I-309/CCL1被识别为表格数据预测认知障碍的可解释候选特征。拟合模型呈现为可审查的阈值表和类条件概率表，具备临床决策支持潜力，但需外部队列验证。

Abstract: Small clinical tabular datasets require interpretable machine learning because deep learning is often impractical and ensemble models can be difficult to inspect. A key pitfall is that statistical significance does not necessarily imply predictive utility. Using data from the Panama Aging Research Initiative--Health Disparities (PARI-HD) cohort (n=165), we implemented a leakage-safe threshold-likelihood Bernoulli/Categorical Naive Bayes (BNB/CNB) classifier. Within every training fold, each continuous predictor was reduced to a supervised chi-square-derived state, while income entered the model through a categorical likelihood. All data-dependent steps were performed within repeated stratified 10-fold cross-validation with 30 repeats. The demographic baseline achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.630 +/- 0.017. I-309 (CCL1) was the dominant incremental feature, increasing AUC by 0.110, with paired DeLong tests yielding p<0.05 in 100% of repeats. In the pre-specified primary analysis, I-309 produced a fixed-partition DeLong p=0.0018, with robustness assessed across 200 random partitions, where the median p-value was 0.0011. Within the exploratory family of 18 candidate markers, I-309 achieved a Benjamini-Hochberg-adjusted q=0.032 on the frozen partition and satisfied q<0.05 in 85% of random partitions, whereas no other marker demonstrated reliable incremental predictive value. Because the fitted model is an inspectable table of thresholds and class-conditional probabilities, these results identify I-309/CCL1 as an interpretable candidate feature for tabular prediction of cognitive impairment, pending external validation.

</details>


### [182] [FCx: An algorithm for finding Feasible Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19383)
*Kleopatra Markou,Vana Kalogeraki,Dimitrios Gunopulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Feasible Counterfactual Explanations (FCx)，一种基于改进变分自编码器 (VAE) 和多因子损失函数的方法，能高效生成兼顾真实性、低成本（接近度与稀疏性）且满足硬/软可行性约束的反事实解释。实验在四个公开数据集上显示其性能达最先进水平并保证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成真实且低成本的反事实，但常忽视可行性，可能建议非建设性或不兼容未来变更的修改（如改变种族以获取工作机会），因此需要显式强制可行性约束。

Method: 基于改进的变分自编码器 (VAE)，采用多因子损失函数优化：成本衡量结合接近度（特征绝对变化量）与稀疏性（变更特征数量）；真实度采用局部异常因子 (LOF) 进行密度估计，确保反事实落在高密度区域；同时引入硬可行性约束（领域知识指定）与软可行性约束（通过因果推断从数据自动推导）。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上进行大量实验，FCx 在多项指标上达到最先进性能，且保证生成的反事实满足可行性要求。

Conclusion: FCx 是首个能高效生成兼具真实性、低成本与可行性的反事实解释方法，通过整合硬/软约束和多因子优化目标，解决了现有方法忽视可行性的关键缺陷。

Abstract: Counterfactual (CF) explanations identify changes that alter an input's classification. While existing methods produce realistic and low-cost CFs, they often fail to ensure feasibility, by suggesting non-constructive modifications or incompatible with future changes (e.g., changing an individual's race to secure a job offer). We introduce a refinement of CF explanations that explicitly enforces feasibility. Our approach is the first to efficiently generate CFs that are realistic, low-cost and feasible. We accommodate both hard feasible constraints, specified by domain knowledge users, and soft feasible constraints, inferred automatically via causal inference from the dataset. Our method, Feasible Counterfactual Explanations (FCx), is based on a modified Variational Autoencoder (VAE) optimized with a multi-factor loss function. We measure the cost of a change based on the absolute change in values (proximity) as well as the number of features changed (sparsity) while realism is measured based on the LOF for density estimation, guaranteeing that CFs reside in densely populated regions. Extensive experiments on four public datasets show that our approach matches state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics while guaranteeing feasibility.

</details>


### [183] [Improving Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning via Selective Reward Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19414)
*Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RSIQL（Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning），一种简单非分层的离线目标条件强化学习方法，通过辅助价值函数识别能推进目标的中间状态并施加奖励激励，解决稀疏奖励与长时程依赖导致的奖励传播延迟问题，在 D4RL 和 OGBench 基准上超越 IQL 并与分层方法性能竞争，同时保持扁平策略结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习面临稀疏奖励与长时程依赖的挑战：目标完成信息与早期关键决策在时间上距离遥远，且离线价值估计引入额外误差。作者从奖励传播视角分析，在风格化的延迟目标设置中表明，目标导向的价值分离可能变得相对于局部估计误差很小，导致学习困难。

Method: RSIQL 引入辅助目标条件价值函数识别离线轨迹中预估能推进目标的中间状态，在这些状态施加奖励激励以提供延迟较小的训练监督。该方法不学习单独的高层子目标策略，保持非分层的扁平策略结构。

Result: 在 D4RL 目标到达基准和 OGBench 上实验表明，RSIQL 平均性能优于目标条件 IQL，并能与分层离线目标条件方法竞争，同时保持简单的扁平策略结构。

Conclusion: RSIQL 是一种简单有效的离线目标条件强化学习方法，通过奖励激励缓解延迟奖励传播问题，无需分层架构即可达到竞争性性能，为解决稀疏奖励和长时程依赖提供了新思路。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning aims to learn policies that reach specified goals, but remains challenging in offline settings with sparse rewards and long-horizon dependencies. In such settings, goal-completion information can be temporally distant from the early decisions that enable success, while offline value estimation introduces additional error. We study this issue from a reward-propagation perspective and show, in a stylized delayed-goal setting, how goal-directed value separation can become small relative to local estimation error. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning (RSIQL), a simple non-hierarchical method that introduces additional reward signals at progress-making intermediate states in offline trajectories. RSIQL uses an auxiliary goal-conditioned value function to identify intermediate states estimated to make progress toward the goal and applies reward stimulation to provide less-delayed training supervision. Unlike hierarchical methods, RSIQL does not learn a separate high-level subgoal policy. Experiments on D4RL goal-reaching benchmarks and OGBench show that RSIQL improves over goal-conditioned IQL on average and achieves performance competitive with hierarchical offline goal-conditioned methods, while retaining a simple flat policy structure.

</details>


### [184] [Bayesian Optimization with Rich Auxiliary Information via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19437)
*Tejus Gupta,Efe Mert Karagözlü,Rohit Sonker,Barnabás Póczos,Jeff Schnieder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用大语言模型(LLM)将丰富的辅助信息(如训练曲线、专家笔记、先验知识)融入贝叶斯优化(BO)，开发了三种方法，在超参数优化和核聚变优化任务上优于标准BO和现有基于LLM的优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实优化问题往往包含比单纯函数评估更丰富的信息(训练曲线、专家笔记、图像、先验知识)，而标准BO难以利用这些辅助信息；LLM擅长处理多模态和文本信息，因此探索LLM能否有效利用这些辅助信息指导优化。

Method: 开发了三种将辅助信息融入BO的方法，利用LLM处理辅助信息(如训练曲线、专家笔记、图像、先验知识)并指导BO的搜索过程。

Result: 在超参数优化基准测试和真实核聚变优化任务中，提出的三种方法均持续优于标准BO和现有基于LLM的优化方法。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明LLM能有效利用丰富的辅助信息增强贝叶斯优化，为解决复杂现实优化问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely used for optimizing expensive black-box functions, yet many real-world optimization problems contain substantially richer information than function evaluations alone. Examples include training curves in hyperparameter optimization, expert notes and images in scientific experimentation, and prior knowledge about where optima may lie. We show that large language models (LLMs) can effectively leverage such rich auxiliary information to guide optimization. Motivated by these findings, we develop three methods for incorporating auxiliary information into BO using LLMs. Across hyperparameter optimization benchmarks and a real-world nuclear fusion optimization task, our methods consistently outperform both standard BO and existing LLM-based optimization approaches. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of LLMs for leveraging rich auxiliary information in BO.

</details>


### [185] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) Improves Classification Accuracy of Bacterial Raman Spectral Data Enabling Portable Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19453)
*Kaitlin Zareno,Jarett Dewbury,Siamak K. Sorooshyari,Hossein Mobahi,Loza F. Tadesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用 Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) 优化器提升拉曼光谱细菌分类模型在有限数据集上的泛化能力，单分割准确率提升高达 10.5%，平均提升 2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗菌药物耐药性预计到 2050 年每年夺走 1000 万生命，资源匮乏地区受影响最重。拉曼光谱有望在数小时内快速、便携地检测抗生素耐药性，但现有算法在有限数据集上难以跨多样人群泛化，且需复杂预处理步骤（如特征提取）以缓解光谱数据质量低的问题。

Method: 引入 Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) 优化器，应用于临床细菌分离株分类任务，通过在多样超参数下增强模型泛化能力，解决现有算法在有限数据集上的泛化不足及预处理复杂的问题。

Result: SAM 在单分割上实现高达 10.5% 的准确率提升，在所有分割上的平均准确率提升 2.7%，显著优于传统 Adam 优化器。

Conclusion: SAM 展现出推进 AI 驱动的拉曼光谱工具临床应用的潜力，为快速、可靠的抗生素耐药性检测提供了新的优化策略。

Abstract: Antimicrobial resistance is expected to claim 10 million lives per year by 2050, and resource-limited regions are most affected. Raman spectroscopy is a novel pathogen diagnostic approach promising rapid and portable antibiotic resistance testing within a few hours, compared to days when using gold standard methods. However, current algorithms for Raman spectra analysis 1) are unable to generalize well on limited datasets across diverse patient populations and 2) require increased complexity due to the necessity of non-trivial pre-processing steps, such as feature extraction, which are essential to mitigate the low-quality nature of Raman spectral data. In this work, we address these limitations using Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) to enhance model generalization across a diverse array of hyperparameters in clinical bacterial isolate classification tasks. We demonstrate that SAM achieves accuracy improvements of up to 10.5% on a single split, and an increase in average accuracy of 2.7% across all splits in spectral classification tasks over the traditional optimizer, Adam. These results display the capability of SAM to advance the clinical application of AI-powered Raman spectroscopy tools.

</details>


### [186] [Enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network by Domain Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19466)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Rose Chong-Wu,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出知识增强农业知情神经网络（KAINN），融入肥料扩散、土壤呼吸、水填充孔隙度等领域知识，在CNN、LSTM、Transformer架构上跨多生长季节评估。结果显示KAINN较纯数据驱动模型和原始AINN具有更低RMSE、MAE、更高R²，学习接口更平滑、物理一致性更强、不确定性更低，证明环境知识融入神经网络能提升N2O排放预测的可靠性、可解释性和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业N2O排放源于土壤属性、气候、生物化学过程与管理措施的复杂交互，且高质量观测有限，导致预测困难。深度学习虽能捕捉非线性关系，但常缺乏物理可解释性且跨环境泛化能力差。

Method: 扩展农业知情神经网络（AINN），构建知识增强农业知情神经网络（KAINN），显式融入肥料扩散、土壤呼吸、水填充孔隙度等领域机制知识。分别采用CNN、LSTM、Transformer作为骨干网络，在多个生长季节及不同输入特征配置下进行对比实验。

Result: KAINN在均方根误差（RMSE）、平均绝对误差（MAE）和决定系数（R²）上普遍优于纯数据驱动模型及原始AINN。解析学习到的接口参数轨迹显示其更平滑、物理一致性更强，且不确定性显著降低。

Conclusion: 将环境领域知识显式融入神经网络架构，能有效提升农业N2O排放预测的可靠性、可解释性和跨条件泛化能力，为可持续农业管理提供更可信的决策支持。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of nitrous oxide (N2O) emissions from agriculture is important for assessing environmental impacts and supporting sustainable farming. However, prediction remains difficult because N2O emissions result from complex interactions among soil properties, climate, biochemical processes, and management practices, while high-quality observations are limited. Deep learning models can capture nonlinear relationships but often lack physical interpretability and may generalize poorly across environmental conditions. We propose the Knowledge-enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network (KAINN), a hybrid neural-mechanistic framework that extends the Agriculture-informed Neural Network by incorporating domain knowledge about fertilizer diffusion, soil respiration, and water-filled porosity. We evaluate KAINN using CNN, LSTM, and Transformer architectures across multiple growing seasons and input-feature configurations. The results show that KAINN generally provides lower root mean square error and mean absolute error and higher R-squared values than purely data-driven models and the original AINN. Analysis of the learned interfaces also shows smoother and more physically consistent parameter trajectories with reduced uncertainty. These findings demonstrate that incorporating environmental knowledge into neural networks can improve the reliability, interpretability, and generalization of agricultural N2O-emission predictions.

</details>


### [187] [Search at the Cost of Sampling: Nearly-Instant Latent Space Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19476)
*Donney Fan,Colin Doumont,Aleksandra Kalisz,Paul Duckworth,Jacob R. Gardner,Henry Moss,Geoff Pleiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于球面域线性模型的贝叶斯优化方法，通过利用高维潜在空间的球面对称性推导出近似闭式解，实现了比现有基线快100倍以上的加速，同时在分子和图像生成基准上保持或提升了性能，使BO成为从头设计流程中实用的即插即用组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在从头发现流程中扮演核心角色，通过大规模生成设计并经虚拟筛选筛选候选者。贝叶斯优化虽天然适配此设定，但其序贯决策的计算开销在虚拟筛选相对廉价时成为瓶颈，限制了BO在大规模生成管线中的实际应用。

Method: 利用高维潜在向量集中于球面域的特性，构建约束于球面的线性代理模型，并推导出利用球面对称性的近似闭式解，分别解决代理建模和采集函数优化问题，从而大幅降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在分子生成和图像生成基准上，该方法比最先进基线实现至少100倍加速，同时性能持平或有所提升，验证了其在真实从头发现管线中的实用性。

Conclusion: 通过球面线性代理模型和闭式解推导，本文消除了贝叶斯优化在大规模生成流程中的计算瓶颈，使其成为从头设计管线中高效、即插即用的优化组件。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly central to many de novo discovery pipelines, in which designs are generated at scale and filtered through virtual screens to determine a set of candidates to experimentally validate. While Bayesian optimization (BO) is a natural fit for this setting, as it uses past evaluations to guide future proposals, the computational overhead required for its sequential decision-making becomes a bottleneck when virtual screens are relatively cheap. We make BO practical in this regime by exploiting the unique combination of a linear model constrained to a spherical domain where high-dimensional latents concentrate. We build off recent work justifying the use of linear surrogates, while deriving nearly closed-form solutions to the surrogate modelling and acquisition problems that exploit spherical symmetry. The result is at least a 100x speedup over state-of-the art baselines, with matching or improved performance across molecular and image generation benchmarks. Altogether, our method makes BO a practical drop-in for de novo pipelines where it was previously too slow to consider.

</details>


### [188] [Sample Count Is Not Enough: Candidate-Generation Strategy Shapes the Energy and Performance of LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19499)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究测试时扩展中候选生成调度对系统成本的影响，发现相同候选数下，串行多次小批量生成比单次大批量生成显著增加能耗和延迟，主张评估应报告生成调度和GPU级系统指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展研究常用候选数N描述推理预算，但N无法反映生成执行方式（批量 vs 串行）对系统成本的影响，需要量化不同生成调度的实际开销。

Method: 在Phi-3-mini和Qwen2.5-1.5B上用GSM8K测试N对准确率的影响；固定N=8，对比四种生成调度（1x8, 2x4, 4x2, 8x1），在A100上测量延迟、吞吐、GPU小时数和总GPU设备能耗，并在多节点及V100/SciQ上验证。

Result: 增大N从1到8分别提升Phi-3-mini和Qwen2.5-1.5B准确率8.4和18.4个百分点；但8x1串行调度比1x8批量调度多消耗4.64-4.86倍能耗，P95延迟高5.77-6.12倍，现象在多节点和V100上一致。

Conclusion: 候选数不足以描述多候选测试时扩展的系统成本；在候选独立且内存允许时，少调用大批量更高效。评估应同时报告候选数、准确率、生成调度及GPU级系统指标。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can improve large language model reasoning by generating and combining multiple candidate responses. In sampling-based methods, the inference budget is often described by the number of generated candidates, N. However, N tells us how many candidates are generated, not how they are executed. The same candidate budget can be produced in one batched generation call or split across several sequential calls with smaller batch sizes. We first study the effect of increasing N on reasoning accuracy using Phi-3-mini and Qwen2.5-1.5B on 500 GSM8K prompts.
